patippuri  
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum perumi cērtta nūṟṟāṇṭākum. innūṟṟāṇṭil tamiḻukku arun toṇṭāṟṟiyavarkaḷ variciyil aṟiñar cāmi. citam paraṉārum oruvar. pakuttaṟivu pakalavaṉ tanti periyāriṉ vāḻkki varalāṟṟi 1939ām āṇṭu tamiḻar talivar eṉum nūliṉi mutaṉ mutalil eḻuti avariṭamē opputal peṟṟu veḷi yiṭṭavar aṟiñar cāmi.citamparaṉār. tanti periyāriṉ talimiyil kalappumaṇam ceytu koṇṭa cīrtirutta muṉṉōṭi.
ivar eḻuti veḷivanta nūlkaḷ 65 eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ pativil kāṇap paṭukiṟatu. itil eṅkaḷ kikkuk kiṭitta nūl kaḷi  kālavariciyil poruḷ vaḻip pirittu cāmi.citamparaṉār nūṟkaḷañciyam eṉum talippil tamiḻ ulakam payaṉpeṟum vakiyil veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷōm. kikkuk kiṭikkap peṟāta ēṉiya nūlkaḷit tēṭiyeṭuttu etirvarum āṇṭil veḷiyiṭa muyaluvōm.
tam eḻutukōli poḻutupōkkukkākavō piḻippukkākavō kiyāḷāta taṉmāṉat tamiḻaṟiñar. tam eḻutti ilaṭciya nōkkuṭaṉ tamiḻarkaḷiṉ nalaṉukkāka eḻutiyavar. taṉittamiḻiyakkam - nītikkaṭci - tirāviṭar kaḻaka īṭupāṭu koṇṭavar. paṉmukap paṭippāḷi. putiya pārviyuṭaṉ tirukkuṟaḷiṉ arumi perumikaḷi āḻntu akaḻntu kāṭṭi yavar. caṅka nūlkaḷil parattiyar naṭpu kaṇṭikkappaṭavilli eṉpatiyum, tirukkuṟaḷ oṉṟiltāṉ mutaṉmutalākap parattiyar naṭpu kaṇṭikkappaṭukiṟatu eṉpatiyum tam nūlkaḷil pativu ceytavar.cittarkaḷiṉ vāḻkki muṟi kaḷum, citta maruttuvattiṉ arumi perumikaḷum ivar nūlkaḷil mikuntu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 
paṭṭamum - pataviyum, celvamum - celvākkum, iḷami yum - aḻakum, poṉṉum - poruḷum mānta vāḻvil niliyaṟṟatu. kalvi aṟivu oṉṟutāṉ nilittu niṉṟu mānta vāḻvil pukaḻ cērppatu  eṉpati  paṭippavar neñcil  patiyum vaṇṇam eḷiya tamiḻil tam nūlkaḷil  pativu ceytavar.
cilappatikāram - araciyal puraṭciyi aṟivuṟutta eḻunta  nūl. maṇimēkali - camutāyap puraṭciyi aṟivuṟutta eḻutappaṭṭa nūl. aimperuṅkāppiyaṅkaḷ pulavarkaḷ pōṟṟum perumikkuriya paḻantamiḻar paṇpāṭṭuc celvaṅkaḷ, cāti vēṟṟumiyiyum, peṇṇaṭimittaṉattik kaṇṭittum. piṟappāl vēṟṟumip pārāṭṭappaṭum koṭumikaḷukku ōṅkik kurali koṭuttavar.
pakuttaṟivup pārviyuṭaṉ paḻantamiḻ ilakki yaṅkaḷ vāyilākat tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkiyiyum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷiyum paṇ pāṭṭiyum, nākarikattiyum teḷivupaṭuttiyavar ivar. muṟpōkku iyakkattiṉ muṉṉaṇit talivar kaḷil oruvarāyt tikaḻntavar. pulami mikka tamiḻ aṟiñarāka iruntapōtum avar paḻimivāti yāka irukkavilli. camutāya māṟṟattiyum pariṇāma vaḷarcciyi yum kaṇakkil koṇṭu tamiḻ ilakkiyatti āyvu ceytavar. piṟpōkku vātikaḷāl uruvākki viṭappaṭṭa pala kaṭṭuk katikaḷi yum kaṟpaṉikaḷiyum ivaruṭiya kaṭṭurikaḷ taviṭupoṭiyākkiṉa.
eṉṟu tiru.ṭi.celvarāj avarkaḷ ippaṭi pativu ceykiṟār. (nūl - cāmi.citamparaṉār - veḷiyīṭu - cākittiya akātemi)
kālamāṟṟatti kaṇakkil koṇṭu paṇṭit tamiḻ ilakkiyatti āyvu ceyyum ivariṉ pakuttaṟivup pārvi aṟiñar ulakam eṇṇattakkatu. ivaruṭiya eḻuttukkaḷil āḻnta camūka akkaṟiyum, tolinōkkup pārviyum paṭintu kiṭakkiṟatu. tamiḻ ilakkiyam, paṇpāṭu, nākarikam, tamiḻarkaḷiṉ toṉmi paṟṟi āyvu ceyya muṉipavarkaḷukku innūṟ kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ peritum payaṉpaṭum eṉṟa nōkkil itaṉit tokuttu veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷōm. itaṉit tokuttum. pakuttum innūṟ kaḷañciyaṅkaḷ veḷivaruvataṟku emakkut tuṇiyāyirunta em patippakap paṇiyāḷarkaḷ, nūlkaḷ koṭuttutaviyavarkaḷ,  kaṇiṉi,  meyppu, accu, nūl kaṭṭamippu ceytu utaviya aṉivarukkum em naṉṟi.	
	- patippakattār

aṇinta viḷakku
(katic ceyyuḷ)
(1944)

















ulakelām pōṟṟum uyarcōḻa nāṭṭil
nilaviya cīrkaranti nīḷnakara vītiyilē
ceṅkatirōṉ tōṉṟic civanta katirparappi
eṅkuviḻi vittālum epporuḷum poṉmayamāyk
kāṇaccey kāliyil kārikiyār kaṇviḻittu
pūṇtirutti vēlikaḷic ceykiṉṟa pōtil
naṭanta nikaḻcciyitu; --nāṭpalavāy makkaḷ
uṭampō ṭuyirnaṭuṅki uṟaṅkāmal vāḻnta
koṭuntuyarkkuk kāraṇamāy vāḻnta koliñaṉ 
piṭipaṭṭa tālmakiḻntē pēciṉār ivikaḷiyē

vāḻtturi
ṭākṭar kaliñar māṇpumiku
mu.karuṇāniti
tamiḻaka mutalvar.
tamiḻaṟiñar kaviñar cāmi. citamparaṉār avarkaḷiṉ kavitit tokuppu nūlāciya aṇinta viḷakku peyaraḷavil aṇinta viḷakkāka iruntālum, uṇmiyil itu aṇiyā viḷakku eṉpati itaṉip paṭikkiṉṟa aṉivarum naṉku uṇarvārkaḷ. iṉṟiya talimuṟiyiṉarkkuc cāmi.citam paraṉār avarkaḷiṉ peyar avvaḷavāka aṟimukamākiyirukka niyāyamilli. tanti periyār avarkaḷip paṟṟi antak kālattilēyē arumiyāṉa oru varalāṟṟu nūli tamiḻar talivar eṉṟa talippil ariya ilakkiyamāka ākkiyavar tamiḻaṟiñar cāmi.citamparaṉār āvār.
puraṭcik kaviñar pāratitācaṉ avarkaḷuṭiya mutal kaviti nūl veḷivantapōtu, ataṟku aṇinturi vaḻaṅkip puraṭcik kaviñariyē aṟimukappaṭuttiyavar kaviñar citamparaṉār āvār. avarum avartam tuṇiviyār tirumati civakāmi citamparaṉārum, kalapput tirumaṇam purintu, antak kālatti lēyē puraṭcikaramāṉa ilaṭciyat tampatikaḷāka pakuttaṟivup pācaṟiyil viḷaṅkiyavarkaḷ.
caṅka ilakkiyattiṟku nikarāṉa intap putumi ilakkiyatti iṉṟiyat tamiḻulakam nallātaravu nalki varavēṟkum eṉṟu tiṭamāka nampukiṉṟēṉ.

āyvuri
makāvittuvāṉ
mē.vī.vēṇukōpālap piḷḷi
āciriyar cāmi.citamparaṉār kalitēruḷḷam vāyntavar. tam nuṇṇaṟivāl nūl pala āyntu cila nūlkaḷukku uri yeḻutiyatuṭaṉ nalla karuttukaḷaṭaṅkiya nūl cilavaṟṟit tāmē iyaṟṟiyum veḷiyiṭṭavar. avartam pulami pōṟṟu taṟkuriyatu.
avar eḻuti vittuc ceṉṟa nūlkaḷuḷ itukāṟum accēṟāka nūlkaḷuḷ aṇinta viḷakku eṉṉum ic ceyyuḷ nūlum oṉṟu. itaṉi avar tam illattaraciyār tiruvāṭṭiyār civakāmiyammiyār ollum vaki muyaṉṟu veḷiyiṭṭiruppatu peritum pārāṭṭattakka ceyalākum.
nūliṉ ceyyuḷ naṭi kaṟpārkku iṉpam payattaṟkuriya tāy amintuḷḷatu. āciriyarē innūlip patippittirun tāl, nūl piṉṉum peruñciṟappeytiyirukkum; patippu muṟi mēlum ceppamuṟṟirukkum.
icciṟu kāppiyattiṟkuk karuvāyaminta kati, aim peruṅkāppiyaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāyt tikaḻntu maṟinta kuṇṭala kēciyiṉ katiyākum. maṟinta nūlukku maṟu vāḻvaḷikka viḻintu āciriyar mēṟkoṇṭa naṉ muyaṟciyiṉ payaṉē innūl.
ceynnaṉṟi maṟavāc centamiḻc cemmakkaḷ itaṉi ollum vaki utavip paravacceyvatit tam kaṭaṉākak koḷvārkaḷāyiṉ, aṭuttu varum patippu piṉṉum ciṟap ceytuvatuṭaṉ, maṟinta karuntaṉatti mīṇṭum peṟṟu makiḻvatu pōṉṟa iṉpatti innūl vāyilākat tamiḻakam peṟum eṉpatu uṟuti.
tamiḻaṟiñar cāmi citamparaṉār pukaḻi icciṟukāppiyam kuṉṟiṉ mītiṭṭa viḷakkup pōlak kulavac ceyyum eṉpatu eṉatu karuttu.
vāḻka citamparaṉār tam vaḷarpuka ḻeṉa vāḻttu kiṉṟēṉ!

aṇinturi
uyartiru ne.tu.cuntaravaṭivēlu
M.A.,L.T.avarkaḷ
tuṇivēntar, ceṉṉi palkalikkaḻakam
eḷiya iṉiya tamiḻ urinaṭi nūlkaḷ pala iyaṟṟi aḻiyāppukaḻ aṭintavar cāmi citamparaṉār. ivarkaḷuṭiya kaviti nūlkaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya aṇinta viḷakku ippōḻutu mīṇṭum cuṭarviṭum pēṟu peṟṟiruppatu peru makiḻccikkuriya tēyākum. paḻiya kuṇṭalakēci kāppiyattit taḻuviya katic ceyyuḷāy amintatu aṇinta viḷakku. āciriyar 1. cintiya kaṇmalar; 2. karukiya kañcam; 3. āccariyap pēriruḷ; 4. pālivaṉa maḻi; 5. coṉṉatē caṭṭam; 6. pūṉi kiyiṟ kiḷi; 7. kuṟṟamuḷḷa neñcu; 8. kacanta vāḻvu; 9. ūr vāykku mūṭiyilli eṉṉum oṉpatu talippukaḷil kaliveṇpā, āciriya viruttam, cintu, paḵṟoṭi veṇpā; āciriyappā mutaliya pala pāvakikaḷālum innūli yāttuḷḷār.
cāmi citamparaṉār eḷiya iṉiya urinaṭiyil maṭṭumē vallavarkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇivantavarkaḷukku ōr iṉpa atircciyāy viḷaṅkuvatu innūl. cāmi citamparaṉār tamatu vaṇṇat tamiḻ pulamiyāl iṉiya tamiḻkkavikaḷ taravallavar eṉpataṟku aṇiyā viḷakkam aṇinta viḷakku.
aṇinta viḷakkiṉ peruñciṟappu muṭiyaracu tolintu kuṭiyaracu amivatēyākum. ataṉiṉum peruñ ciṟappu kaḷḷaṉik kātalittu kaṇavaṉākak koṇṭa iḷavaraci avaṉik koṉṟapiṉ akkuṭiyaraciyum potuvuṭimi aracāka māṟṟa muṉivatēyākum.
cāmi citamparaṉār. centamiḻp paṟṟil ciṟantavar eṉpa taṟkuc ciṟantatōr eṭuttukkāṭṭu kātal moḻiyilum varum kaṉṉittamiḻiṉ pukaḻēyākum. eṭuttukkāṭṭu:
centamiḻt tēṉūṟṟum ceñcoṟ kaviyāṉa
antac cilappatikāram aḷikkiṉṟa
cintāmaṇiyē! ciṟantamaṇi mēkaliyē!
muntit tamiḻmaṟavar pōrvīram muṟṟum
iṟavāmal meyppulavar iṉcollāṟ ceyta
puṟanāṉūṟē! pukaḻ patiṟppattē eṉ
akanāṉūṟē! naṟṟiṇiyē! yaruñcoṟ
pukalum kuṟuntokiyē! aiṅkuṟu nūṟē! nal
paripāṭalē! tamiḻar paṇṭināḷ māṇpum
ariyatamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ aminta nila vaḷamum
muttumuttāka muḻutun toṭutturikkum
pattuppāṭṭē! eṉ paḻantamiḻiṉ tēṉperukkē!
ellāp poruḷum itaṉpāluḷ vitaṉpāl
illāta epporuḷum illiyāl eṉṟu pukal
poyyā moḻiyār pukaṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷiṉ
meyyāṉa mupporuḷē! muttamiḻiṉ melloliyē
uṉṉālē yaṉṟō uyirvāḻvēṉ nāṉulakil
maṉṉavaṉē! eṉṟaṉ maṉampaṟitta mārutamē!
aṇinta viḷakkiṉ āciriyar tammuṭiya nūliṉ taliviyi iḷavaraciyi nūl palavum kaṟṟaṟintāḷ. nuṭpa matiyuṭiyāḷ eṉṟu pōṟṟukiṟār. appaṭippaṭṭavaḷ ārāyāmal oru kaḷḷaṉik kātalippatu ēṉō? nūl pala kaṟṟālum nuṇṇaṟivu peṟṟālum kāmattiṟkuk kaṇ illi pōlum! ataṉ viḷivākap paṭṭaṟintē pakuttaṟivāḷiyum payaṉpeṟa vēṇṭum pōlum.
eḷiya iṉiya tamiḻcceyyuḷ iyaṟṟutalilum vallava rāy viḷaṅkum cāmi citaramparaṉār putiya uvamikaḷip pukal vatilum kālatti etiroli ceykiṟār eṉpatu teḷivu. ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭu :
kiyāl tirukiyatum kaṭṭuṇṭa taṇṇīrip
peyyum kuḻāypōlap peṇṇamutu taṉkiyik
kaṇkaḷilē vittuk kacakkiyatum tāriyāy
maṇmītu nīrcinta niṉṟu maṉaṅkuḻintāḷ.
maṟinta cāmi.citamparaṉār maṟiyāta tamiḻp pukaḻukku eppoḻutum uriyavar. inta aṇinta viḷakku. ataṟku ōr aṇiyā viḷakku āvatāka!

ciṟappuri
ṭākṭarci.pālacuppiramaṇiyaṉ,
em.ē., em.liṭ., piec.ṭi.,
tamiḻttuṇip pērāciriyar,
ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakam.
tamiḻaṟiñar tiru.cāmi citamparaṉār tamiḻiṟku niliyāṉa toṇṭuceytu maṟintavar; tām iyaṟṟiya takuti vāynta nūṟkaḷiṉ vāyilāka iṟantum iṟavātu vāḻpavar. pakuttaṟivuk koḷkiyuṭaṉ, ciṟanta kuṟikkōḷōṭu tamiḻ vaḷarccikkāka allum pakalum uḻittavar. avaruṭiya eḻutukōl tamiḻiṉ pala tuṟikaḷiyum paṟṟi niṉṟatu; payaṉuṟa eḻutiyatu. avartam paṭippukkaḷil avartam ariya uḻippu muyaṟciyiṉiyum, uyariya uṇmiyāṉa ārāyccip pōkkiṉiyum oruṅkē kāṇalām. caṅka ilakyi māyiṉum cari, iṭikkāla pakti ilakkiyamāyiṉum cari piṟkāla ilakkiyamāyiṉum cari, avartam eḻutukōl naṭuvu nilimiyuṭaṉ uṇmikaḷi ārāyntu kaṇṭatu. ciṟanta ilakkiya āciriyariṭam kuṭi koṇṭirukka vēṇṭiya paṇpukaḷ aṉittum avarkaḷpāl kuṟivaṟa nirampiyiruntaṉa. avartam uyariya uḻippum viḻumiya toṇṭum tamiḻiḷiñar pōṟṟi mēṟkoḷḷat takkaviyākum.
aṟiñar tiru. cāmi ciramparaṉār avarkaḷ iyaṟṟi, itu vari veḷivarāta aṇinta viḷakku eṉṉum katic ceyyuḷ ippōtu veḷivarukiṉṟatu.
aimperuṅ kāppiyaṅkaḷil oṉṟāṉa kuṇṭalakēciyiṉ katiyiṉi appaṭiyē eṭuttukkoṇṭu, perumpālum kaliveṇpāvilum, ciṟupāṉmi cintu aciriya viruttam āciriyappaḵṟoṭi veṇpā mutaliya yāppu naṭikaḷilum āciriyar ik kaviti nūliṉi yāttuḷḷār.
nūl, cintiya kaṇmalar, karukiya kañcam, āccariyap pēriruḷ, pālivaṉa maḻi, coṉṉatē caṭṭam, pūṉi kiyiṟ kiḷi, kuṟṟamuḷḷa neñcu, kacanta vāḻvu, ūr vāykku mūṭi yilli eṉṟu oṉpatu pakutikaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu in nūliṉuṭ ceṉṟu nayaṅkaṇṭu teḷivōm.
kavimaṇi avarkaḷ,
uḷḷat tuḷḷatu kaviti - iṉpam
uruveṭuppatu kaviti
teḷḷat teḷinta tamiḻil - uṇmi
terinturippatu kaviti
eṉṟu kuyippiṭṭiruppataṟkēṟpa āciriyar kaviti, nūliṉ palaviṭaṅkaḷilum uḷḷattu uṇarccikaḷit teḷḷat teḷiyak koṇṭu teḷinta nīrōṭṭam pōl celkiṉṟatu. uṇarcci vēkamum. kaṟpaṉi nayamum, karuttuvaḷamum, vaṭivac ciṟappuṅkoṇṭu kaviti vīṟārntu niṟkiṉṟatu.
āciriyariṉ varuṇaṉit tiṟam, ucciyilē kārmēkam vāḻa uṭampiṉilē paccic civappu mayila mañcaṇiṟap paṭṭuṭuttik kātilē tōṭaṇintu kiyil vaḷitarittu vīti viḷakkēpōl vīcum paṇi pūṇṭu miṉṉal koṭipalavum mētiṉiyil vantulavik kaṉṉal moḻipeytu kātaṟ payir vaḷarttu niṉṟu naṭappaveṉa, nēriḻiyār paṟpalarum oṉṟu kuḻumiṉār. (pa.61)
eṉṟu peṇkaḷi varuṇikkum pakutiyāl veḷippaṭu kiṉṟatu.
uvami yaḻaku,
viṇmīṉ puṭicūḻa veḷḷi nilāvīcit
taṇmatiyam oṉṟu tavampukuntu niṟpatupōl
kārikiyāḷ niṉṟutaṉ kaṇmalarkaḷ vīcuvati
vīraṉun tāṉkaṇṭāṉ	(pa:64)
veppattiṟ paṭṭurukum veṇṇeyip pōluḷḷam
ippaṭi ninturukac ceytāḷ, eḻilaraci	(pa:66)
nīrpukuntu pāluḷḷē niṉṟu maṟintatupōl
vīraṉavaṉ eṉṉuḷ virivāyk kalantuviṭṭāṉ	(pa:89)
mutaliya pakutikaḷil viḷakkamuṟukiṉṟatu.
maṇamakkaḷi vāḻttum makkaḷ,
kātalarkaḷ nīṭuḻi vāḻntu kaḷippuṟuka!
ētum kuṟiviṉṟi iṉpak kaṭalpaṭika!
makkaṭ perumpēṟṟi eyti makiḻvuṭaṉē
ekkālum vāḻka	(pa:116)
eṉa vāḻttukiṉṟaṉar. vāḻttiyalil kaviñar marapip pōṟṟak kāṇalām.
iḷavaraci cuntaraṉip pukaḻumpōtu tamiḻpperu nūlkaḷōṭu uvamittup pēcutal āciriyartam tamiḻppaṟṟi viḷakkuvatākum. (pa. 121, 122)
paḻamoḻikaḷi āciriyar iṭiyiṭiyē kiyāḷumtiṟam
kuṟṟamuḷār neñcu kuṟukuṟukkumāmaṟṟu	(pa:126)
kāmālik kaṇṇaṉukkuk kaṇṭavelām mañcaṇiṟam	(pa:128)
eṉṟa aṭikaḷāl viḷaṅkum.
tirukkuṟaḷip pala viṭaṅkaḷil appaṭiyē akaḻnteṭuttu aḻakuṟak kiyāḷukiṉṟār (64, 136)
iṟutiyil iḷavaraci,
maṉṟil uṟivīr! matiyuṭiyīr kēṇmiṉō!
iṉṟu mutalāka yāṉum iṉiya tamiḻt
teṉṟal vaḷarntōṅkat teḷḷamutam nāṭellām
ōṭuṟavē ceyvēṉ! ulakellām centamiḻi
nāṭumpaṭiceyvēṉ! nāḷum itaṟkuḻippēṉ! 	(pa:146)
eṉṟu kūṟuvatu, āciriyar koṇṭuḷḷa uḷḷak kiṭakki yākum.
kaviti vaḷam niṟinta eḻil nūl aṇinta viḷakku
itaṉi veḷiyiṭum aṉṉiyām tirumati civakāmi citamparaṉār avarkaḷ tam āruyirk kaṇavar niṉivu pōṟṟi, paṇpu pōṟṟi, toṇṭupōṟṟi vāḻpavar. aṉṉār viṭṭuc ceṉṟa paṇikaḷit toṭarntu iṭiyaṟātu iyaṟṟivarupavar. avar tūya paṇi vāḻka, velka eṉa vāḻttuvaṉ.
tamiḻ kūṟu nallulakam ivviṉiya kaviti nūli vāḻtti varavēṟṟu ilakkiyap payaṉ tuykkumāka!
tamiḻakam	ci.pālacuppiramaṇiyaṉ
ceṉṉi 29 	8.10.1973

muṉṉuri
katit tōṟṟam
nīṇṭa nāḷāka eṉakkōr āval uṇṭu. oru katic ceyyuḷ eḻutavēṇṭum eṉpatē atu. anta āvaliṉ eḻucciyē aṇinta viḷakku, intak kati muḻuvatum eṉatu kaṟpaṉiyaṉṟu. paḻaṅkatiyum, eṉatu kaṟpaṉiyum kalantuḷḷa katiyē itu. ik katikku aṭippaṭi kuṇṭalakēci; atu paḻaṅkati.
aimperum kāppiyaṅkaḷ cilappatikāram, cintāmaṇi, maṇimēkali, kuṇṭalakēci, vaḷiyāpati eṉpavi. ivaṟṟuḷ kuṇṭala kēciyum oru kāppiyam. āṉāl it tamiḻnūl ippoḻutu kiṭikka villi it tamiḻk kāppiyattiṉ uṇmik katiyum iṉṉa teṉṟu uṇaramuṭiyavilli. pāḷi moḻiyil uḷḷa tēri kāti eṉṉum nūlilum. tamma pātā; aṅkuttaraniyākā eṉṉum nūlkaḷilum kuṇṭala kēciyiṉ kati kūṟappaṭṭiruppatāka moḻikiṉṟaṉar. camaṇacamaya nūlākiya nīlākēci eṉṉum nūlil 286 ām ceyyuḷiṉ uriyilum kuṇṭalakēciyiṉ kati kūṟap paṭṭirukkiṟatu
irācakirukattu maṉṉaṉatu amiccaṉukku oru makaḷ. avaḷ peyar pattiri. avaḷ takka paruvamuṟṟāḷ. oru nāḷ taṉ māḷikiyiṉ māṭiyiṉ mēl ulavikkoṇṭiruntāḷ. aracaṉatu koliyāḷikaḷ oru kaṭṭaḻakaṉik kolik kaḷattiṟku aḻittuc ceṉṟaṉar. ati avaḷ kaṇṭāḷ. avaṉ mītu kātaluṟṟāḷ. avaṉ vaḻippaṟi ceyta kuṟṟattiṟkāka maṉṉaṉāl kolit taṇṭaṉi vitikkappaṭṭavaṉ; aracaṉuṭiya purōkitaṉ makaṉ.
avaṉiyē maṇakkavēṇṭum eṉṟu pattiri piṭivātam ceytāḷ. amiccaṉ, koliyāḷikaḷukkuk kikkūli koṭuttāṉ, tiruṭaṉi uyir piḻikkac ceytāṉ.
kaḷvaṉukkup pattiriyi maṇamuṭittuk koṭuttāṉ tiruṭaṉum pattiriyum palanāḷ iṉpuṟṟu vāḻntaṉar. orunāḷ ūṭaliṉ pōtu, pattiri, muṉpu nī kaḷvaṉ aṉṟō eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ. avaṉ avaḷ taṉṉi ikaḻntatāka eṇṇic ciṉaṅkoṇṭāṉ. āyiṉum appoḻutu avaṉ ciṉatti veḷiyiṭātu maṟittu vāḻntiruntāṉ.
orunāḷ avaṉ, pattiriyiṭam eṉ uyirik kāppāṟṟiya teyvatti vaṇaṅka, aṇmiyilirukkum malimukaṭṭiṟkuc celkiṟēṉ. nīyumvaruka eṉṟāṉ avaḷum uṭaṉpaṭṭāḷ. iruvarum maliyucciyi aṭintaṉar. aṭintatum tiruṭaṉ, nī eṉṉik kaḷvaṉeṉṟu ikaḻntāy aṉṟō! ātalāl iṉṟu uṇṉik kollap pōkiṟēṉ uṉ vaḻipaṭu teyvatti vaṇaṅkikoḷ eṉṟāṉ. avaḷ tiṭukkuṟṟāḷ. taṟkolliyi muṟkolla vēṇṭum eṉpati uḷḷattil eṇṇiṉāḷ. avaṉ āṇiyi ēṟṟavaḷ pōl naṭittāḷ. uṉṉiyaṉṟi eṉakkut teyva milli; uṉṉiyē valam vantu  vaṇaṅkuvēṉ eṉṟāḷ. avaṉi valam varupavaḷ pōl piṉpuṟamāka vantu, avaṉik kīḻē taḷḷik koṉṟāḷ.
piṉṉar avaḷ ulaki veṟuttuc camaṇa matat tiṟcērntu tuṟavu pūṇṭāḷ. tuṟaviyāṉavuṭaṉ tali mayir kaḷiyap paṭṭatu. āyiṉum mīṇṭum talimayir muḷittuc curuṇṭu kāṇappaṭṭatu. ātalāl ivaḷukkuk kuṇṭalakēci eṉṟa peyar vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. kuṇṭalakēci yeṉṟāl curuṇṭa mayiriṉi yuṭiyavaḷ eṉpatu poruḷ.
ituvē nīlakēciyiṟ kāṇum katiyākum. ikkatiyiṉi, in nūliṉ kati aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭatu. āyiṉum, mutaliliruntu iṟutivariyiṟ pala māṟutalkaḷiyuṭiyatu. itaṉ katic curukkattiyum kīḻē tarukiṟōm.
tañci nakar aracaṉ makaḷ kōmaḷavalli. avaḷē paṭṭattiḷavaraci tamiḻk kalvi niṟintavaḷ. cuntaranātaṉ eṉṉum tiruṭaṉik kaṇṭāḷ; avaṉ mēl kātal koṇṭāḷ. tiruṭaṉi aracaṉ vicārittuk kolit taṇṭaṉi tantāṉ; kōmaḷavalli avaṉiyē maṇakkap piṭivātam ceytāḷ; aracaṉ avaṉuṭiya taṇṭaṉiyi māṟṟiṉāṉ. avaṉic cēṉit talivaṉ ākkiṉāṉ.
maturi maṉṉaṉ piratāpaṉ. avaṉ kōmaḷavalliyiṉ poruṭṭu, tañci nakar mītu paṭiyeṭuttu vantāṉ. cuntaranātaṉ avaṉi etirttut tōlviyuṟac ceytāṉ. ivaṉuṭiya veṟṟikkup paricāka kēmaḷavalliyi maṇam ceytu koṭuttāṉ maṉṉaṉ.
kaḷvaṉum iḷavaraciyum orunāḷ tōṭṭattil kātal moḻipēcik kaḷittiruntaṉar. iruvarum taṅkaḷ kātal veḷip paṭap pāṭikkoṇṭintaṉar, kōmaḷavalli, taṉ kātal mikutiyāl, nī iṉṉum  uṉ tiruṭṭiyum, koliyiyum maṟantuviṭavilli eṉṟu pāṭiṉāḷ, kaḷvaṉ kaṭuñ ciṉamuṟṟu, ati maṟittuk koṇṭu, poyyaṉpu kāṭṭi vāḻntu vantāṉ.
kōṭi nāḷ vantatu. veyyiliṉ koṭumiyāl kōmaḷavalli tuṉpuṟuvatu kaṇṭu, paḻitīrkka atuvē paruvam eṉṟu tiruṭaṉ karutiṉāṉ. avaḷuṭaṉ nīla malikkuc ceṉṟu taṅkiṉāṉ
orunāṉ maṟimati nāḷil iruvarum oru malic cikaratti aṭintu uriyāṭik koṇṭiruntaṉar. poḻutu pōyiṟṟu. kōmaḷavalli kaḷvaṉi uṟiviṭattiṟku aḻit tāḷ. avaṉ taṉ uḷḷatti veḷiyiṭṭu, uṉ vaḻipaṭu teyvatti vaṇaṅkik koḷ eṉṟāṉ. avaḷ nīyē vaḻipaṭu teyvam eṉṟu valam vantāḷ. kālam pārttu avaṉip paṭukuḻiyil taḷḷiṉāḷ - kaḷvaṉ māṇṭāṉ.
piṉṉar iḷavaraci ūr vantu cērntāḷ. aracaṉ vayatu mutirnta kāraṇattāl aracāṭciyiliruntu vilaka muṭivu ceytāṉ. yāriṭam araci oppuvippatu eṉṟu avi yiṉari viṉava, kōmaḷavalliyē aracukkuriyavaḷ eṉṟaṉar.
kōmaḷavalli aracēṟka maṟuttāḷ. kuṭi aracē ciṟanta teṉṟu aviyiṉarukkuk kūṟiṉāḷ. aṉivarum oppiṉar aracaṉ vilakiṉāṉ. kuṭikaḷuḷ oruvaṉāṉāṉ kōmaḷavalliyum kuṭikaḷuḷ oruttiyāṉāḷ aracu kuṭiyaracāyiṟṟu.
ituvē aṇinta viḷakkiṉ katic curukkam. itaṉiyum, kuṇṭalakēciyiṉ katiyiṉiyum oppu nōkkiṉ, vēṟupāṭu viḷaṅkum.
ikkati innūlil oṉpatu pirivākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. opvoru piriviṉ ciṟanta karutti viḷakkum aṭikaḷil uḷḷa coṟkaḷiyē appirivukaḷukkut talip peyarāka  amikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. katippakutic curukkam eṉṉum talippiṉ kīḻ, ovvoru pakutiyiṉ katiyiyum curukkamāka eḻutiyuḷḷēṉ. atip paṭitta piṉ nūlip paṭittāl, uyttuṇarviṉṟik katiyi aṟintu paṭikkalām.
peyarkkāraṇam
aṇinta viḷakku innūlukku poruttamāṉa peyarāka irukkumeṉṟu karutiyē appeyar koṭuttēṉ.
veṇpāviṉ, neṭu veṇpāṭṭu, allatu paḵṟoṭi veṇpāvē nāṉkiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa aṭikaḷiyuṭiyatu. tol kāppiyattil, neṭu veṇpāṭṭukku 12 aṭikaḷ elli kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
neṭu veṇpāṭṭē munnālaṭittē eṉpatu tol kāppiyam. (tol, cey. 470) piṟkāla nūlākiya yāpparuṅkalak kārikiyil, neṭu veṇ pāṭṭiyē paḵṟoṭi veṇpā veṉak kūṟiṉar. piṟkāla ilakkaṇa nūlār palarum ip peyariyē kūṟiṉar.
kārikiyil paḵṟoṭi veṇpāvukku, aṭi variyaṟi kūṟappaṭa villi. pala aṭikaḷiyuṭiyatu paḵṟoṭi veṇpāveṉṟē kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu.
aṭi palavāyc, ceṉṟu nikaḻva paḵṟoṭiyām eṉpatu kāriki.
tolkāppiyattil aṭivariyaṟi kūṟiya pāvukkup piṟkālattār aṭivariyaṟi kūṟāmal viṭṭa kāraṇam, putiyaṉa pukutal ākumeṉṟē karutu kiṉṟaṉar cilar. ivvāṟu aṭi palavuṭiya veṇpāvukku ilakkiyam kiṭikka villi. ātaliṉ itaṉi veṇpā vakiyiṟ cērttal poruntātu. veṇpāttaḷi viraviya maṟṟōr pāṭṭu kali veṇpāvākum. kaliveṇ pāvit tolkāppiyar kaliveṇpāṭṭeṉak kuṟippar.
kaliveṇpāṭṭu eṉpataṟkut tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭum ilakkaṇam :-
oru poruḷ nutaliya veḷḷaṭi iyalāṟ
ṟiripiṉṟi varuvatu kaliveṇpāṭṭē
eṉpatu.
cilappatikārattil uḷḷa kaṉāttiṟam uritta kāti yeṉpatu, ittakiya veṇpāttaḷi peṟṟa pāṭṭāl ākiyatu. ak kātiyiṉ iṟutiyil aṭiyārkku nallār eḻutiya uriyil appāṭalik kaliveṇpāṭṭu eṉṟē kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār.
itu kaṉaveṉṉum poruḷiyē karutātu. mālati katiyiyum, tēvanti katiyiyum kalantu kūṟiṟṟēṉum, uṟuppaḻiviṉṟi naṭantatēṉum, mun nāṉkaṭiyiṉiṟantu varutalāṉ neṭuveṇ pāṭṭākātu kaliveṇpāṭṭāta luṇarka!
eṉpatu aṭiyārkku nallār uri ātalāl nām veṇpāttaḷi piṟaḻāmal varukiṉṟa 12 aṭikaḷukku mēṟ paṭṭa pāṭali kaliveṇpāṭṭu eṉṟē aḻippōm.
ātalāl in nūliṉ perumpālāṉa pāṭṭukkaḷ kaliveṇ pāṭṭukkaḷ ākum. iṉṉum, viruttappā, āciriyappā, putiya vakiyil uḷḷa cila pāṭal kaḷum itil kalantuḷḷaṉa.
eḷiya naṭiyum, iṉiya cantamum, putiya poruḷum uṭiya pākkaḷ pala tōṉṟavēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉ karuttu. aṟiñarkaḷum, itu tamiḻaṉṉikkup putup paṇi eṉṟu karutu kiṉṟaṉar. in nūlum, in nūṟ pāṭalkaḷum, in nūṟ karuttum, centamiḻaṉṉikku oru putup pūṇāka irukkalākātā? eṉṟa āvaluṭaṉēyē yāṉum innūli eḻutiṉēṉ. innūl ciṟitaḷavāvatu tamiḻ vaḷarccikkut tuṇipuriyumāyiṉ. atuvē eṉ karuttukkuc ciṟanta paricu kiṭittatākak koṇṭu makiḻvēṉ.
aṉpaṉ,
cāmi.citampaṉār.


cāmi. citapparaṉāriṉ aṇinta
viḷakku

katip pakutic curukkam
1
cuntaranātaṉ oru peruṅ kaḷvaṉ; avaṉ aṭikkaṭi tañci nakarik koḷḷiyiṭṭu vantāṉ.
tañci nakar maṉṉaṉ tayāniti. avaṉ maṉivi taṇ kōti tēvi. avarkaḷukku orē peṇ. avaḷ peyar kōmaḷavalli. aḻakiṟ ciṟantavaḷ; tamiḻk kalviyiṟ tērntavaḷ. avaḷē paṭṭattiḷavaraci.
kaḷvaṉip piṭikkak kāvalarkaḷ pala nāṭkaḷ muyaṉṟaṉar. iṟutiyil piṭipaṭṭāṉ. tañci nakarppuṟattil oru periya ēri. ataṉ kariyil oru tāḻaṅkāṭu. atil oḷintiruntāṉ cuntaranātaṉ. kāvalar talivarkaḷuḷ oruvaṉ iruḷappaṉ. ivaṉ kaḷvaṉip piṭittāṉ. kāvalarkaḷ kaḷvaṉukku vilaṅ kiṭṭaṉar. tañci nakariṉ terukkaḷiṉ vaḻiyē iḻuttu vantaṉar. potumakkaḷ ak kāṭciyik kaṇṭaṉar. kaḷvaṉip paṟṟip palap pala pakarntaṉar,. avaṉuṭiya tōḻarkaḷ palar avaṉi mīṭka vaḻiyiṉṟi varuntic ceṉṟaṉar.
araṇmaṉi māṭiyil mēl iḷavaraci, tōḻikaḷuṭaṉ viḷiyāṭik koṇṭiruntāḷ. apapoḻutu, kaḷvaṉi, araṇmaṉi vīti vaḻiyāka aḻittu vantaṉar. iḷavaraci teruvil eḻunta muḻakkattik kēṭṭāḷ. viyiṭṭi viṭṭu ōṭi vantu teruvi nōkkiṉāḷ. kaṭṭaḻakuṭiya cuntaranātaṉik kaṇṭāḷ; kātal koṇṭāḷ; kaḷvaṉum aṇṇāntu pārttāṉ; aracakumāriyik kaṇṭāṉ; iruvar kaṇkaḷum oṉṟupaṭṭaṉa.
kāvalar talivaṉ, aracaṉ muṉ kaḷvaṉik koṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ. aracaṉ iṉṟu iravu ciṟiyil vittiruppīr. nāḷi namatu capikkuk koṇṭu varuka  eṉṟu paṇittāṉ. kaḷvaṉip piṭitta iruḷapṉukkup paricu pala vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ. kaḷvaṉi aṉṟiravu ciṟiyil vittaṉar; kāvalar kāttu niṉṟaṉar.
2
ciṟikkuḷḷirunta cuntaranātaṉukku uṟakkam varavilli. araca kumāriyiṉ tōṟṟam avaṉ neñci viṭṭu akala villi; avaḷ uruveḷit tōṟṟaṅ kaṇṭu uḷḷaṅ kuḻin tāṉ; uṇmiyāka avaḷē vantu viṭṭatāka eṇṇiṉāṉ; eḻuntāṉ; naṭantāṉ; āṭiṉāṉ; pāṭiṉāṉ; pataṟiṉāṉ. kāvalar avaṉip pittaṉeṉṟu ikaḻntaṉar. kaḷvaṉuṭiya kaṭuneñcam kārikiyiṉ nōkkāl kaṉivaṭintatu.
araca kumāriyum, araṇmaṉiyil kātalāl kalaṅki niṉṟāḷ. kaḷvaṉuruvik karuttiṟ patittāḷ  avaṉi maṇappa tevvāṟu eṉṟu karutic cōrntāḷ. tōḻiyar avaḷ nōy kaṇṭu varunti niṉṟaṉar. avaḷ nōyi nīkkap palavita paṇi viṭikaḷ ceytu niṉṟaṉar.
3
poḻutum pularntatu. kaḷvaṉ vaḻakkip pārkkum poruṭṭu ūrār tiraṇṭaṉar; aracaṉuṭiya avik kaḷatti aṭintaṉar. amiccarkaḷum aracavi vantu amarntaṉar.
tayāniti aṉṟu naṭipeṟap pōkum, kaḷvaṉ vaḻakkip paṟṟik kōmaḷa vallikku viḷampiṉāṉ. avaḷiyum aḻittuk koṇṭu capikku vantāṉ; ariyācaṉattil amarntāṉ. iḷavaraciyum, oru puṟattil uṭkārntāḷ.
kāvalarkaḷ kaḷvaṉik koṇṭu vantu aracaṉmuṉ niṟuttiṉar. aracaṉ kaḷvaṉuṭiya kuṟṟaṅkaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉāṉ. kaḷvaṉ aracaṉi etirttu, nīyum eṉ pōṉṟa tiruṭaṉ; kolikāraṉ; nammiruvaḷ vēṟṟumiyilli eṉṟāṉ. aracaṉ cīṟṟaṅ koṇṭu avaṉi veṭṭutaṟku vāḷeṭuttāṉ.
āymatiyaṉ eṉpavaṉ mutal amiccaṉ. avaṉ virin teḻuntu maṉṉaṉ kiyip piṭittuk koṇṭāṉ. tiruṭaṉ moḻiyiṉ karutti naṉṟāka uṇarntu koḷḷu vōm; piṟaku taṇṭaṉi koṭuppōm eṉṟāṉ. maṉṉaṉum ciṉantaṇintu uṭkārntāṉ.
āymatiyaṉ kaḷvaṉi nōkki. aracaṉum uṉ pōṉṟavaṉ eṉpatu evvāṟu? viḷaṅkac col eṉṟāṉ.
kaḷvaṉ nāṉ tiruṭukiṉṟēṉ; eṉṉip piṭikka varuvōrik kollu kiṉṟēṉ. celvar poruḷiyē koḷḷi koḷvēṉ; taṟkāppukkākavē koli ceyvēṉ. aracaṉ uḻippāḷar poruḷi variyiṉ vaḻiyākak koḷḷi koḷḷu kiṉṟāṉ;pōr purivataṉ mūlam koli ceykiṉṟāṉ. itu koliyaṉṟō? eṉṟāṉ.
amiccaṉ aracaṉ ceyal, nāṭṭu nalaṅ karutiyatu; āṉṟōrāl oppuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu; nūlātaravuḷḷatu; veḷippaṭiyākac ceyyappaṭuvatu. uṉ ceyal ellōrālum ikaḻappaṭuvatu eṉṟāṉ. kaḷvaṉ itaṟku maṟumoḻi kūṟāmal niṉṟāṉ.
aracaṉ, kaḷvaṉukkut taṇṭaṉi yātu? eṉṟu avi yiṉarik kēṭṭāṉ. kolit taṇṭaṉiyē takkatu eṉaṟu ellērum kūṟiṉar.
veṟṟinātaṉ eṉṉum amiccaṉ ivaṉ iḷiñaṉ añcā neñcaṉ; pakuttaṟivāḷaṉ; ātalāṟ kolla vēṇṭām. ivaṉic cīrtirutti nallōṉākki nāṭṭut toṇṭukkup payaṉpaṭuttik koḷḷalām eṉṟāṉ. aviyiṉar ivvamiccaṉ moḻiyi yikaḻntaṉar.aracaṉum avamatittāṉ  kaḷvaṉukku maraṇa taṇṭaṉi eṉṟu muṭivu kūṟiṉāṉ maṉṉaṉ. 
maraṇa taṇṭaṉi eṉṟa muṭivu kēṭṭatum kaḷvaṉ tikittāṉ; iḷavaraci tiṭukkiṭṭāḷ; aviyōr makiḻntaṉar. iḷavaraciyiṉ kaṇkaḷum, kaḷvaṉ kaṇkaḷum cankittaṉa. kaḷvaṉ cōrvuṟṟuk kīḻē viḻuntāṉ. iḷavaraciyum pēccu mūccaṟṟāḷ; cōrntāḷ; cāyntu viṭṭāḷ. aviyōr ikkāṭci kaṇṭaṉar; viyantaṉar iḷavaraciyi araṇmaṉikku eṭuttuc ceṉṟaṉar. avi kalintatu.
4
kaḷvaṉ peṟṟa kaṭuntaṇṭaṉiyāl iḷavaraci āvi tuṭittāḷ; cōrntu kiṭantāḷ. aṉṟu pakalum, iravum aracaṉum, araciyum tōḻiyarum avaḷ pakkattiliruntu  varuntiṉar; tuyaram taṇiyap paṇi viṭikaḷ pala purintaṉar; maruttuvarkaḷ palar maruntaḷittaṉar. avaḷ tuṉpam māṟa villi.
maṟu nāḷ poḻutu viṭintatu. aṉṟutāṉ kaḷvaṉik koli ceyyum nāḷ. iḷavaraci, tāṉ kaḷvaṉ mītu koṇṭirukkum kātali mella veḷiyiṭṭāḷ. atu kēṭṭa aṉivarum tikittaṉar. aracaṉ nī tiruṭaṉ mītu vitta āci kātal alla; kāmamē; ati maṟantu viṭu eṉṟāṉ taṇkōti tēviyum evvaḷavō kūṟiṉaḷ. kōmaḷavalli  kātal vēṟaṉṟu; kāmam vēṟaṉṟu; iraṇṭum oṉṟē; kaṭṭaḻakaṉi maṇakkāviṭil uyir vāḻēṉ eṉṟu muṭivākak kūṟiviṭṭāḷ. taṇkōti tēviyum, kaḷvaṉuṭiya kolit taṇṭaṉiyi māṟṟumāṟu aracaṉi vēṇṭiṉaḷ.
aracaṉum maṉamiraṅkiṉāṉ. cuntaranātaṉuṭiya kolit taṇṭaṉiyi māṟṟi viṭṭatāka oru ōli yeḻutiṉāṉ. ati oru tōḻiyiṉ kiyiṟ koṭuttāṉ; kolik kaḷattil uḷḷa atikāriyiṉ kiyiṟ cērkkum paṭi paṇittāṉ. tōḻiyum, avvōliyi vāṅkik koṇṭu virin tōṭiṉāḷ.

5
nalla naṭuppakal. kolik kaḷattil kaḷvaṉ oru mēṭiyiṉ mēl niṟuttappaṭṭuḷḷāṉ. pala makkaḷ kuḻumiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. cilar avaṉ māḷap pōvatu karuti iraṅkukiṉṟaṉar, palar avaṉ māḷap pōvatu karuti makiḻcci yaṭikiṉṟaṉar. ic camayattil, kolit taṇṭaṉiyi niṟivēṟṟum atikāri tiruṭaṉ taliyit tuṇikkumāṟu koliyāḷikaḷukku aṟivittāṉ. oru koliñaṉ kaḷvaṉ kaḻuttiṟku nēraka vāḷi ōṅkiṉāṉ.āṉāl veṭṭu viḻumāṟu vīcavilli.
iccamayattil kollātē! nillu! kolit taṇṭaṇiyi māṟṟiṉāṉ maṉṉavaṉ eṉṟu kūvik koṇṭu ōṭi vantāḷ oru peṇ. avaḷ aracaṉ kaṭṭaḷiyi atikāri yiṉ kiyiṟ koṭuttāḷ. atikāri kolit taṇṭaṉiyi niṟuttiṉāṉ.kaḷvaṉi aracavikkuk koṇṭu pōyiṉar. itu kaṇṭu potu makkaḷ viyappuṟṟup paṟpala moḻintaṉar.
aracavi kūṭiyiruntatu. palarum cūḻntiruntaṉar. aracaṉ  nēṟṟu veṟṟinātaṉ kūṟiya moḻikaḷ eṉ maṉattiṟ patintaṉa. ātalāl kolit taṇṭaṉiyi māṟṟiṉēṉ; ik kaṭṭiḷiñaṉic cīrtiruttalāmeṉṟu karutukiṉṟēṉ. uṅkaḷ eṇṇam yātu? urimiṉ eṉṟāṉ veṟṟinātaṉ maṉṉaṉ ceykiyi ātarittāṉ. ivvīraṉi namatu cēṉit talivaṉākkutal nalam  eṉṟāṉ.
āymatiyaṉ ivaṉ nal vaḻiyil naṭappatāka uṟuti kūṟiṉāl avvāṟē ceyyalām eṉṟāṉ.
maṉṉaṉ kaḷvaṉi nōkki, uṉ eṇṇam eṉṉa veṉṟāṉ. kaḷvaṉ nāṉ vaṟumiyāl koṭumi cey tēṉ. iṉi oḻuṅkāka naṭappēṉ. eṉakkuk koṭukkum vēliyit tiṟampaṭac ceyvēṉ eṉṟāṉ.
aracaṉ appōtē kaḷvaṉic cēṉit talivaṉāk kiṉāṉ; varici pala tantāṉ. immuṭivic cilar veṟuttaṉar; cilar pukaḻntaṉar. avi kalintapiṉ aracaṉ iḷavaraciyiṭam ceṉṟāṉ. innikaḻccikaḷi aṟivittāṉ; avaḷ tuṉpam nīṅki yiṉpa muṟṟāḷ.
6
maturi nakar maṉṉaṉ piratāpaṉ; maṇamākātavaṉ. avaṉukkup palar peṇ koṭukka muṉvantatum, maṇam puriya maṟuttiruntāṉ. avaṉ oru nāḷ taṉiyāka araṇmaṉit tōṭṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟāṉ. aṅkē oru mēṭiyil uṭkārn tāṉ. pakkattu marakkiḷiyil iru kuyilkaḷ iruntaṉa. avi kātal moḻi pecik kaḷittaṉa. atu kaṇṭa maṉṉaṉ oru puttuṇarcci peṟṟāṉ aṉṟu mutal avaṉ maṉatil kavali kuṭi koṇṭatu. ak kavali mukattil veḷip paṭṭiruntatu.
amiccarkaḷ avaṉuṭiya mukac cōrvu kaṇṭaṉar. avaṉukku maṇamuṭippatē avaṉ kavali tīrvataṟku vaḻi yeṉṟu muṭivu kaṇṭaṉar. aracaṉi yaṭintaṉar. nīṅkaḷaṉi varum vantatu eṉ karuti? urimiṉ! eṉṟāṉ maṉṉaṉ. mutalimiccaṉ cukuṇaṉ, kōmaḷa valliyiṉ aḻakiyum, aṟiviyum eṭuttuk kūṟi. avaḷē uṉakkēṟṟa peṇ avaḷi maṇantāl nī makiḻcciyaṭivāy eṉṟāṉ maṉṉaṉum maṇam purintukeḷḷa oppiṉāṉ.
piratāpaṉukkup peṇ koṭukkumāṟu tayānitikku amiccarkaḷ kaṭitam viṭuttaṉar. pala nāṭkaḷ kaṭanta piṉ, tayānitiyiṉuṭiya maṟumoḻi kiṭittatu; piratāpaṉukkup peṇ koṭukka maṟuttāṉ. uṭaṉē piratāpaṉ ciṉaṅ koṇṭāṉ.
amiccarkaḷ cūḻcciyiṉpaṭi, piratāpaṉ paṭi tiraṭṭic ceṉṟu tañci nakari muṟṟuki yiṭṭāṉ.
iccamayam, tayāniti araca maṉṟattiliruntāṉ amiccar kaḷum. piṟarum cūḻntiruntaṉar. oṟṟarkaḷ ōṭivantu piratāpaṉ paṭiyeṭuttu vantirukkum ceytiyik kūṟiṉar. atu kēṭṭu aracaṉ tikittāṉ; amiccarkaḷ viḻittaṉar.
cuntaranātaṉ eḻuntāṉ. nāṉ ceṉṟu piratāpaṉiyum, avaṉuṭiya paṭikaḷiyum muṟiyaṭittu varukiṟēṉ eṉṟāṉ. aracaṉum makiḻntu viṭi tantāṉ. cuntaranātaṉ, taṉ cēṉi kaḷuṭaṉ nakarppuṟatti yaṭintāṉ piratāpaṉ paṭiyuṭaṉ pōr toṭuttāṉ piratāpaṉum, avaṉ paṭi vīrarkaḷum tōṟṟup puṟamutukiṭṭaṉar.
cuntaranātaṉiṉ veṟṟiyik kēṭṭu maṉṉavaṉum makkaḷum mikiḻntaṉar. maṉṉavaṉ  cuntaranākaṉ peṟṟa veṟṟikkup paricāka kōmaḷavalliyi maṇañ ceytu  koṭukkap pōkiṟēṉ. avaḷum ivaṉ mēl ārāta kātal koṇṭāḷ eṉṟāṉ palarum naṉṟē yeṉṟaṉar; cilar iḵteṉṉa viparītam! eṉṟu veṟuttaṉar.
tirumaṇattukku nāḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. tamiḻ muṟippaṭi cuntara nātaṉukkum iḷavaracikkum kaṭimaṇam nikaḻntatu maṉṉaṉ makiḻntu paricu pala vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ makkaḷum makiḻntaṉar. kaḷvaṉum iḷavaraciyum kalantu makiḻntu vāḻntu vantaṉar.
7
araṇmaṉic cōli. atil oru tāmari pūtta taṭākam; ataṉ kariyil oru maṇṭapam; ammaṇṭapattil ōrmēṭi; am mēṭiyil cuntaranāṉum iḷavaraciyum vīṟṟiruntaṉar. kātalmoḻi pala pēcik kaḷittaṉar kāviṉ vaḷaṅkaḷik kaṇṭumakiḻntaṉar. oruvari oravar pārāṭṭip pukaḻntaṉar. cuntaranātaṉi oru pāṭal pāṭavēṇṭiṉaḷ. kaḷvaṉ iḷavaraciyiṉ kātalē taṉṉi nalvaḻippaṭuttiṟṟu eṉṉum karuttup paṭap pāṭiṉāṉ. piṟaku avaḷum taṉ kātali veḷiyiṭṭup pāṭiṉāḷ avaḷ taṉatu pāṭṭil nī eṉṉi uṉmaṉattil tiruṭi vittirukkiṉṟāy! nī ciṟitu nēram eṉṉi viṭṭup pirintiruntālum, appirivu eṉṉik kollukiṟatu ātalāl uṉ paḻantoḻili iṉṉum nī viṭavilli eṉṟa karutti veḷiyiṭṭāḷ. tiruṭaṉ avaḷ kātaṟ karutti uṇara muṭiyāmal ciṉamuṟṟāṉ. avaṉ taṉ ciṉatti veḷiyiṭāmal maṟittuk koṇṭāṉ. piṉṉum ciṟitu nēram avarkaḷ aṅkē yiruntuviṭṭup poḻutu paṭṭatum araṇmaṉikkup pōyiṉar.
8
aṉṟu mutal kaḷvaṉ kalakkamuṟṟāṉ. avaṉuṭiya paḻaya ceyalkaḷip paṟṟiya niṉivukaḷ vantuviṭṭaṉa. piṟar taṉṉi nōkkum pōtellām. kaḷvaṉeṉṟu karuti nōkkuvatākavē niṉittāṉ piṟar pārkkum pōtellām nāṇit tali kaviḻntāṉ. avaṉukku araca vāḻvu puḷittuviṭṭatu; kōmaḷa valliyiṉ mēl veṟuppuṟṟāṉ; avaḷip paḻivāṅkutaṟkuk kālaṅkarutiniṉṟāṉ.
kōṭi nāḷ piṟantatu. veyyiṟ koṭumi tāṅka muṭiyavilli veppattiṉ mikutiyāl iḷavaraciyiṉ uṭampil koppuḷaṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa ituvē avaḷi paḻivāṅkac camayam eṉṟu karutiṉāṉ. cuntaranātaṉ iḷavaraciyi nōkki kōṭik koṭumi nīṅka, nīlakirikkuc celvōm eṉṟāṉ avaḷum uṭaṉpaṭṭāḷ iruvarum pōtiya ēvalar kaḷuṭaṉ nīlamaliyi aṭintaṉar. aṅkōr māḷikiyil taṅkiṉar.
palanāṭkaḷ nīlakiriyil vāḻntaṉar. oru amāvācināḷ aṉṟu māli cuntaranātaṉum iḷavariciyum oru kuvaṭṭi yaṭintaṉar. kīḻē kiṭukiṭu paḷḷam. avviṭattil uṭ kārntu iruvarum kātal moḻi pēciṉar iruḷ cūḻntatu atu kaṇṭu iḷavaraci nēramāyiṟṟu viṭutikkuc celvōm eṉṟāḷ cuntaranātaṉ nī eṉṉik kaḷvaṉ kolikāraṉ eṉṟu cerukkāl ikaḻntāy! ātalāl uṉṉik koṉṟu eṉ veñciṉattit tīrttukkoḷḷap pōkiṟēṉ. ippoḻutē uṉatu vaḻipaṭu teyvatti vaṇaṅkikkoḷ eṉṟāṉ.
iḷavaraci, eṉṉik kolla niṉikkum iv vañca kaṉi nāṉ kolvēṉ  eṉṟu muṭivu ceytu koṇṭāḷ. kaḷvaṉi nōkki,nīyē eṉ vaḻipaṭu teyvam! uṉṉi yaṉṟi vēṟu teyvamaṟiyēṉ! eḻuntu nil; valam vantu vaṇaṅku kiṟēṉ eṉṟāḷ. tiruṭaṉum eḻuntu niṉṟāṉ. avaḷ avaṉic cuṟṟi varumpōtu, takka camayam pārttup paṭukuḻiyil taḷḷi viṭṭāḷ avaṉum āveṉṟalaṟi vīḻntu māṇṭāṉ.
itu nikaḻnta piṉ iḷavaraci tāṅkā-t tuyaruṟṟāḷ vāy viṭṭu araṟṟiṉāḷ. araṟṟik koṇṭē, avviṭatti viṭṭu iṟaṅki vantu koṇṭiruntāḷ. itankuḷ ēvalarkaḷ avaḷi yum cuntaranātaṉiyum tēṭi vantaṉar. avarkaḷ. iḷavaraciyiṉ aḻukural kēṭṭu avaḷi yaṭintu, aḻittuc ceṉṟaṉar. naṭanta nikaḻcaciyi ēvalarkaḷum aṟintu tuṉpuṟṟaṉar. aṉi varum aṉṟiravu naṭantu tañci yaṭintaṉar. iḷavaraciyiṉ ceyti tañcinakar muḻuvatum paraviṭṭatu.
9
tañci nakar muḻuvatum iḷavaraciyi paṟṟiyē pēccu; aracaṉip paṟṟiyum, kaḷvaṉip paṟṟiyum palar pēciṉar aracaṉum, araciyum, iḷavaraciyiṉ nilikuṟittu varuntiṉar vayatu mutirnta maṉṉaṉ āṭciyil veṟuppuṟṟāṉ; araca viyik kūṭṭiṉāṉ. aviyiṉari nōkkināṉ taḷḷāta paruvamuṟṟēṉ. kōmaḷavalli kaḷvaṉi maṇantu kaṭum paḻikku āḷāyiṉaḷ. iṉi aracāḷuvatu yār? uṅkaḷ viruppam eṉṉa? eṉṟāṉ.
mutal mantiri kōmaḷavalliyē aracāḷalām avaḷ kaḷvaṉik koṉṟatu vīrac ceyalē ataṉāl paḻiyoṉṟu milli. eṉṟāṉ ellōrum amiccaṉ moḻiyi ātarittaṉar.
maṉṉaṉ kōmaḷavalliyiṉ mukatti nōkkiṉāṉ. avaḷ nāṉ kātali nampik keṭṭēṉ. ulivāykku mūṭiyuṇṭu; ūr vāykku mūṭiyilli aḻiyāp paḻipūṇṭa nāṉ araciyāka virumpavilli. kuṭikaḷē tāṅkaḷ virumpum oruvarit tērnteṭuttu āṭci puriyalām. kuṭi aracu ciṟanta aracu eṉṟāḷ. 
avaḷ moḻiyi aracaṉum amiccarkaḷum, potu makkaḷum oppuk koṇṭaṉar.
uṭaṉē aracaṉ, nīṅkaḷē aracāḷuṅkaḷ! nāṉ uṅkaḷuḷ oruvaṉāyiruntu aracāṭcikku utavi ceykiṟēṉ eṉṟu kūṟa, kōmaḷa valliyum iṉṟumutal nāṉum kuṭikaḷuḷ oruttiyāy iruppēṉ. tamiḻ moḻikkum tamiḻ makkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkum uḻippēṉ. aracukkum utavuvēṉ eṉṟāḷ.
makkaḷum, taṅkaḷuṭ cilari oru kuḻuvākat tērn teṭuttaṉar. akkuḻuviṉaruḷ oruvaṉit talivaṉākkiṉar kuṭi aracukku vēṇṭiya caṭṭa tiṭṭaṅkaḷamittaṉar. kuṭiyaracāki iṉpuṟṟu vāḻntu vantaṉar.

Foreword by the Authour to
“Anintha Vilaku”

(Transl ation)
It was my ambition to create a good story in mellodious verse.  aṇinta viḷakku (Blown out lamp) is the out- come of it. This is not my pure imaginary creation. It is a subtle mixture of an ancient story and mine own stuff. The basis of this story is adapted from Kundalakesi, an epic.
Kundalakesi is one among the five celebrated great Tamil epic Poems, the four other being, Silapathikaram, Chintamani, Manimekali and Valayapathi. The Story proper of Kundalakesi, as narrated in that great epic is not at avilable anywhere in the world, as the Poem is a lost treasure. Still, the outline and synopsis of the story are sid to contin in Theri Kaathi, Dhamma Patha and Angutharaniyaka which are of Pali literature. Besides, in the commentary of verse 286 in Nilakesi, a Jainist Philosophy, the following summary of Kundalakesi is manifested.
The Prime-minister of the kingdom of Raja Graham had a beautiful grown-up dughter named Baddhiri. One day as The was strolling on the balcony of her mansion she beheld a handsome youth led away by golers to the gallows. Instantly she fell in love with him. That unhappy fellow who was the target of this “Love at first sight” had been the son of the king’s Priest. the Criminal was condemned to death for High way Robbery.
As Badhiri expressed her desire to marry this condemned man above as her spouse. and admently stated that she would never marry anyone else, the minister greased the palms of the golers and saved the dacoit from the halter.
Later the prime - minister gave Baddhiri in marriage to that thief. They lived happily for many days. But as illuck would have it, one night in a lighter vein she remarked that after all he was a Robber! Though he pretended to have swallowed the insult, he never forgot or forgave that humiliation.
Later, one day he invited his wife to accompany him to a hillock, where he was proceeding, he climed, to offer his grateful and thank-giving prayers to the deity that saved him from death. As she readily consented and went with him to the top of the hillock he shouted at her and cried, “Now fall in worship to any deity that you hold sacred, as you are going to meet with your end at my own hands in a few minutes. “How adacious had you been to call me a Robber at my own face!” She was much too shocked at this unexpected outburst. However, wise, clever and intelligent that she was, she decided to outwit him. “Whoever be a greater god than a husband to a chaste wife?” asked she and cajoled him, “Pray, permit me to circumambulate you, my Lord, and offer my humble prayers at your feet” As he nodded in consenm, she went round him, and kicked him down into the steep valley to his death. Thus the rogue met with the poetic justice.
As Baddiri felt disgusted with the way of this mundane world, she embraced jinism, became a Sanyasini, and had her head completely tonsured according to Jain tradition and rites. But lo! Her hirlock agin grew lustily in all magestic waves and curls. She was hence called “Kundalakesi”, a term which denotes curly hirlocks.
This is the story which Nilakesi offers us. Our story in this book is broadly based upon it. But there is a vast difference throughout. Synopsis of our story that follows is as here under: 
the crown princess  Komalavalli is the only issue of the king of Thanjavur, She is a scholer in Tamil. She met with Sundaranathan, a thief, and fell in love with him. As the king condenned him to death for his crime, the princess intervened to express her desire that she would ever remin a spinster if he is executed, as she had already given room in her bosom as her only husband. The king remmitted the sentence, and made him the chief of the Army.
Prathapan, the king of Maduri, in his lust towards Komalavalli invaded on Thanjavur, sundaranathan met him on the battle-field and won his victory. The king, immensely pleased at this valour of his army- chief, gave his dughter in marriage to him.
One day as the thief and the princess were mutually adoring each other in amorous speech and verse, Komalavalli expressed that he has not yet given up robbery nor murders. He was highly incensed. But he never showed his mind out.
As summer came, Komalavalli was found suffering from the agonies of summer - heat. He thought it was the proper moment for him to avenge himself. He took her to some hill resort.
On a moonless night as the couple were spending happy moments on the top of a hill, the robber burst out his anger and ordered her to offer her last prayers to her deity. She went round him with folded hands calling him as the only deity and at the right moment pushed him down the precipice to his doom.
Later as she arrived home, her old father due to advance in age offered to abdicate his throne in favour of his dughter. But she refused the crown. she favoured only the Republican form of government, which was readily accepted by all. the king after his abdication had himself become an ordinary citizen, so also his dughter, and the kingdom became a Republic.
This is the summary of the story of this book. A comparison would reveal the difference between this story and that of Kundalakesi.
This story is divided into nine parts in this book. This heading of each part is named in the every words that are employed in the verses of the climax of such parts. Under the heading of Synopses of story proper in each part I have given the outlines of the narration. If the book is read in the footsteps of such synopses, the whole story will reel in easy flow.
The Title
I have chosen the title “Blown out lamp” in earnest and hope this would justify the cuse. Corresponding verse to denote this title is found in the ninth part of this book. Komalavalli’s lamentation - “I am indeed a blown out lamp!” is called out from there to adorn the title of this book.
Komalavalli was indeed a  flame of glory in the fields of culture, knowledge, wisdom, and beauty. Her lusture diminished as she married a thief. The flame of her happy days was after all blown out. This is subtly expressed in her lamentation. Hence, the title of this book is shaped from the heroine’s tragie life proper.
The book
Some cheif aims of this book are:
1. 	If there is no good political system of govement, no country, no citizen can ever live in peace. Therefore it is that there are constitutions framed in each land.
2. 	The reason why citizens resort to robbery, larceny. murder etc. is poverty and unemployment. Therefore, it is the duty of the govt. to erase them out.
3. 	It is a mistake to call love ‘divine’, and lust “beast-like” Love is a common phenomenon, whereas lust is a unique feature. The essence of love is lust. Lust, in general, means happiness. Hence, it is not proper to prise one and condemn the other totally.
4.  	 However much a lady is lettered and learned she is disappointed in her love or lust. Men can easily dupe them or cheat them. Women-flolk are seldom equipped with clirvoyance.
5.  	 It is the duty of the government, of a state to convert the criminals into law-abiding citizens.
6.	 Those who are indulged habitually in criminal dealings since their childhood cannot be easily corrected. Bad habits never get erased quickly.
7. 	 Republican form of government is by far better than one-man rule.
These ideas are specially conveyed in this book- Besides, some ideals and imaginary description of natural events also find their place.
Poems
The verses of this book generally follow the pattern set by Post Bharathi in his “Kuyil Paatu” and Bharathi Dasan in his “Sanjeevi Parvatham” Following verse are composed in the metrics of “Venba”
Generally a Venba contins four lines only, but those exceeding four and reaching 12 lines are called “Nedu Ven Paattu” or “Pahrodi Venba” neṭu veṇ pāṭṭē munnālaṭittē (tol, cey, 470) The Tholkappiam sets 12 lines as maximum limit of such lyrics.
Yapparungalak Kaariki calls “Nedu Ven Paattu” as “Pahrodi Venba” Later grammarians also follow this rule.
The Kaariki does not prescibe the maximum number of lines of a Pahrodi Venba, though it mentions that such Venbas contin more number of lines or verses: aṭi palavāyc ceṉṟu nikaḻva paḵṟoṭiyām (kāriki)
Whereas Tholkappiam prescribed the maximum limit, later grammarians conveniently omitted the same to accommodate the later innovations of succeeding generations. Hence it is permissible, they clim. There are no literature avilable to show Venba of many lines. Therefore, it is not right to include the same in Venba series. But there is a lyric called “Kali Venba” which qualifies the qualities of Venbas. Tholkappiar calls Kali Venba as Kaliven Paattu”.
1.  	 Definition given in Tholkappiam for Kaliven Paattu” is as follows:
oru poruḷ nutaliya veḷḷaṭi iyalāṟ
ṟiripiṉṟi varuvatu kaliveṇ pāṭṭē.
2. There is a sub story under the title kaṉāttiṟam uritta kāti in Silappathikaram composed in Venba lyrics. Towards the end of that story Adiyarkku Nallar (a commentator) writers of say that poem is “Kaliven Paattu”. He says: - itu kaṉaveṉṉum poruḷiyē karutātu, mālati katiyiyum, tēvanti kati yiyum kalantu kūṟiṟṟēṉum, uṟuppaḻiviṉṟi naṭantatēṉum mun nāṉkaṭiyi ṉiṟantu varutalāṉ neṭu veṇpāṭṭākātu kali veṇ pāṭṭāta luṇarka.
3. 	 This is Adiyarkku Nallar’s commentary. Hence, we call any stanza contining more than 12 lines, but composed in the metrics of Venba, as a Kali Venba.
Many lyrics and verse of this book are of Kaliven Paattu metrics. Besides, Viruthappa, Aasiriyappa, and modern verses also are employed here.
That easy style, sweet melody and modern meanings should adorn any poem is my dictum. Learned authors also consider such poems as new service to the Mother Tamil. How I hope that this book of mine contining such poems and ideals will be a new jewel in the crest of sweet Tamil. I wrote this only with such a cherised desire. If this book in its own humble way assists the growth of Tamil, I shall be highly elated and delighted as the one who gined the great prize.
Anban
SAMI. CHIDAMBARANAR

STORY OF  “ANAINTHA VILAKU”
By Sami. Chidambaranar
Sundaranathan was a great robber. He was often plundering the city of Tanjore.
Dayanidhi was the king of Tanjore. His wife was Thankothi Devi. They had an only dughter - Komalavalli. She was of great beauty and was well educated in Tamil method of studies. She was the crown princess.
The king’s men took great efforts for many days to catch the theif. At last their efforts fructified. On the outskirts of the city of Tanjore ran a big river, on the banks of which had grown great forest, dark with bushes and trees. There Sundaranathan was hiding himself. At last Irulappan one of the armdty  chiefs of the king traced the thief and arrested him.
The arrested robber was manacled adtnd dragged through the wide streets of the great city of Tanjore. People witnessing the scense spoke a thousand trivial things about the thief. His companions could not liberate him. Unable as they were they returned to the forest with grief.
On the terrace of the palace the princess was playing with her friends. Attracted by the noise of the people she left her play and came running to the street. There she saw the handsome Sundaranathan; he saw her; their eyes met; that one moment of ecstacy! Oh! love had born at first sight.
The army chief brought the thief before the king. The king ordered him to be put behind the bars that night and to be brought before him the next day. Irulappan was given great many rewards. Sundaranathan was locked in the prison; Vigilant soldiers were placed to watch him.
II
Sundaranathan was sleepless in the prison; the beautious form of the princess occupied his mind and eyes; he felt as if she hereself was present there; he sung with all the fanciful and mad delight of a lover; the soldiers lughed at his mad behaviour. His heartlessness left him becuse he had given his heart to a beautiful graceful damsel whom he loved with all frenzy.
The princess, state in the palace was none the better. She dreamt of the thief. She was disturbed and dissipatted by the thought of her marriage with him may remin a dream for ever. Her friends were all in great sympathy of her state and strived hard to alluviate her grief. But instead it grew.
III
The next day dawned beautifully but it was a pinfull beauty to the lovers. Immense throngs were pouring in at the kings court to witness the trial. The king came with his entourage and occudtpied the torone. He was sorrounded by wise ministers.
Komalavalli was also present there becuse the king had told her about the trial and had taken her to the court.
The thief was produced before the king. The king levelled many charges aginst him. But the robber unafrid  retorted “Oh king! you are also a thief; murderer. There is no difference between us” he sid. The king infuriated took his sword to behead the thief.
But Aaimathiyan. the chief minister of the king stopped the king; and admonished, “Oh king; let us know what the robber implies and then punish him.” His rage alluviated the king settled in his royal seat.
“Oh thief” interrogated Aaimathiyan “how there come no difference between the king and you? tell me”
Replies the robber. “Yes. Your majesty! I commit thievery; and I kill those who try to trap me. Moreover I rob only the rich and I kill only in self defence. But the king, What he does? He robs the toiling peasants and labourers in the name of taxes; he commits homicide in wars. Is it not robbery? Is it not murder?”
The learned counsellor sid “But oh thief! the king’s acts are aimed at the country’s welfare. It is accepted by the wise and admired by many a man. It is done in public. But your act is despised by all.
The thief could not answer; he remined silent. Terible wave of silence reigned at the moments that glided away.
At last the king consulted the counsellors as to what punishment is to be given. It was agreed that he should be beheaded.
But vetrinathan, a learned counsellor of the court interfered and sid “your majesty! this man is young and brave; moreover he is wise. Kill him not. Reform him and make him usefull to the country’s service”. But the people rediculed the learned words of Vetrinathan. The king also turned deaf ears of his words.
The death Sentence was confirmed.
On the pro nonuncement of the death sentence the robber was bewildered but the courtiers were happy. The eyes of the thief and the princess met. The thief fell down in great grief. The princess was also deeply grieved and she swooned. The courtiers wondered at the happenings. The court was dispersed and the princess was taken to the palace.
IV
The punishment pronounced upon the thief plunged the princess in great sorrow. Her heart throbbed at the thought; She swooned. Everybody in the palace including the king and Queen attended her. They tried their best to heal her ailment but it did not.
The next day’s sun rose above the rough mountins and the vast seas. That day had been fixed for the beheading of the robber. The princess, encouraged by her pure love, exhibidtted it to her parents. They were greatly bewildered. “Your love on the robber is not love” sid the king. “But it is mere lust. Forget it” Thankothi Devi also persuaded her. But Komalavalli would not give up. She argued that love and lust are the same and ultimately sid that she would not live if she was not married to her hand some lover.
Then the Queen also persuaded the king to change the death sentence.
The king took great pity at the lover of his beautiful dughter. He sent a signed message, altering the death sentence. A girl of the palace hurried to the execution place.
V
It was noon. The scorching sun was at the zenith. The robber was standing on a rised place. A great mob was around him excited. Some people pitied him and others were happy that the robber is going to die. The king’s officer has given the command. The killer rised the sword over the thieves neck.
All of a sudden a cry stopped him. A girl was running towards the killer shouting “Stop! kill him not! The king has altered the sentence!” The message was handed over to the official. The execution was stopped. The robber was taken to the king’s court. The people were astonished by the happenings of the day.
In the court. the king declared. “I am impressed by Vetrinathan’s words. Hence only we stopped the execution. We think it befitting that this young man may be reformed. What do you opine?
Vetrinathan approved of the king’s action and suggested that Sundaranathan may be made the chief of the army.
Aaimathiyan approved of the king’s action and suggested that Sundaranathan may be made the chief of the army.
Aaimathiyan also sid that he would accept the same provided the robber promises that he behave in a good manner. Then the king questioned the robber as to his intention.
The robber replied “Your majestry, it was my poverty which compelled me to commit robbery. From now onword I would be a different man; and I would do unto perfection whatever you command”
At once the robber was made the commander of the king’s force. The king  gave him fabulous prizes. Some of the courtiers hated the king’s decision but some others appreciated it.
The king went to the princess and told her about the happenings; her sorrow vanished like snow before the sun.
VI
Maduri - it was an adjacent territory to Tanjore and its king was Prathabhan. Many a king was ready to marry their dughters to him. But he did not assent to many. One fine evening he went to the garden in his palace. He sat on one of the many beautiful benches in the park. In a nearby treebranch were perched two lovebirds. They were wooing. On seeing the love of the birds, a sudden flash of a feeling had come into him. Henceforward he became sorrowful.
The ministers in his court found that the only penance to his newborn sorrow is marying a beautiful girl to him. They approached the king and Sugunan, the Chief minister told him about the beauty and intelligence of Komalavalli. The king accepted to marry her.
Message was sent to Dayanidhi. After waiting for many days they got the reply from Dayanidhi declining the offer.
Prathabhan got enraged. He invaded the city of Tanjore with his forces.
Dayanidhi convened the court immediately. The spies of the king informed him of the unpending invasion. He was astonished. Even the ministers could not utter a syllable.
But Sundaranathan  rose up. “I shall go with my forces and vanquish the proud alien king.” he swore. The king also happily granted permission. The armies faced each other on the battle field in the outskirts of the city. Prathabhan wad defeated and his forces were driven away.
The king and the people were filled with joy on hearing about the great victory. He was given a grand welcome in the court. The king declared “I give my dughter Komalavalli in marriage to Sundaranathan as a trophy of his great victory. She also is in deep love with him.” Many a man appreciated the decision; a few people hated this.
The royal wedding was celebrated in a grand manner on the fixed day. The king confered many boons upon the people. The united lovers revelled in great pleasure
VII
There was a pleasant park behind the palace. In the park was there a beautiful pool/ with blooming lotuses. The lovers were there, speaking about their love, their dreams and so on. The princess requested him to sing a song. He sang a song in which he sid that the pure love of the princedts  alone did make him a reformed man.
Then Komalavalli also sang a song. She sang “Oh my dear! you’ve robbed my heart. I cannot bear even a little while of parting. So you’ve n’t left your old profession of robbery”
But the old robber could not catch the meaning. He got his feelings lacerated. He got offended. But he did not show out his anger. Then they left the park and went to the palace.
VIII
From then onwards the robber got perplexed in mind. thoughts of his old guilt tormented him. He found suspicion in the eyes of the others whenever they locked at him. He was hunted by an ever-growing sense of guilt. The royal life became sour to him. He began to hate Komalavalli. He awaited a chance to take vengence upon her.
The Summer season also had come. The princess got inflammation of skin owing to excessive heat of the climate. Nathan thought that apt chance had come to execute his vengence. He proposed that they may go to Nilgiris where it would be pleasant to be in the summer season. She also accepted.
They reached Nilgiri with their attendants. They settled in a palace. Many days glided by. It was a newmoon day. On that evening they reached a mount. Below the mount was a deep canyon. It became dark. The princess proposed that they may go to the palace. But the thief sid “you humiliated me. You called me a thief your vanity. I am going to kill you. You now itself pray your god.”
The princess thought in her mind that she would kill the trecherous robber. So she sid to the thief, “you are my Deity. I do not pray any god but you. Please stand up. I will come around you thrice and pray you.”
The robber also stood up. She came behind as on the pretence of prayer and pushed him from behind. He was thrown into the deep canyon and was elimenated for ever.
But the princess could not bear the sorrow of it. She began to cry. The attendants found her in that state. They took her to the palace. The next day they left for Tanjore.
The city was plunged in grief. The people were all in sympathy for the pitiful plight of the princess. The aged king also became fed up with his kingly status. He convened the court and declared “My dear citizens! I have grown old! The Princess became the most pitiable woman becuse she married a robber who is to rule this country. We shall like to know your opinion.”
The chief minister argued “Komalavalli may ascend the throne. Her act of killing the thief was an act of courage. It is no sin.” All the courtiers accepted the view.
The king stared interrogatively at her face. She spoke “I am doomed becuse I did belive in false love. “The mouth of a furnace can be sealed but  one cannot shut the mouth of the gossipping world. Moreover, people’s government is the best government. People can rule themselves in a better way than we rule. So let this land become a peoples land, a republic.”
The king and other accepted her words. The king expressed his wish that he would be one of the citizens; helping the rule of the poeples republic. Komalavalli also nodded her assent to be a citizen helping the republic to thrive, the republic of the great Tamils.
The people formed in a great republic; elected a repreentative assemply, made one among them the chief and lived very happily thence forward.

eḷḷaḷavum aiyam illikāṇ - eṉṟu
iyampi niṉṟaṉar varuntiyē.
innilattil vāḻviṉṟi eṉṟum perumtuyaril
maṉṉuvōr tuṉpamelām māṇṭu maṟintoḻiya
nallaṟaṅkaḷ ceytiruntāl, nāḷpalavāyc cūriyaṉṟaṉ
pollāk kaṭuveyilāl  poṉṟum niliyaṭinta
nelliṉ payirum niliyaḻinta puṟpūṇṭum
cilleṉṟu mēkam ceṟintu maḻipoḻiyat
taṅkaḷ tuyaram tavirntu talinimirntē
eṅkum pacumi eḻilviḷaṅka niṟpatupōl
āṉanta māki aṉivōrum vāḻttuvār.
īṉaṉ eṉakkūṟi eḷḷuvār yārumilar;
ēḻik kuṭimakkaḷ iṉpam peṟavurittal
pāḻuṭampi nalluṭampāyp paṇṇum vaḻi, inta
uṇmiyik kāṇāṉ, ulakōr paḻimoḻiyat
tiṇmi uṭiyāṉ tiruṭaṉ koliñaṉāy
vāḻntāṉ; aṟattiṉ vaḻiyaṟiyāṉ; eṉṟu tuyar 
āḻntu palacolli niṉṟār aṟivuṭiyōr.
peṇkaḷ pēccu
ucciyilē kārmēkam vāḻa uṭampiṉilē
paccic civappumayil mañcaṇiṟap paṭṭuṭuttik
kātilē tōṭaṇintu kiyil vaḷitarittu
vīti viḷakkēpōl vīcum paṇipūṇṭu
miṉṉal koṭipalavum mētiṉiyil vantulavik
kaṉṉal moḻipeytu kātaṟ payirvaḷarttu
niṉṟu naṭappaveṉa, nēriḻiyār paṟpalarum 
oṉṟu kuḻumiṉār; oṇpuruva villiṉilē
kaṇṇampu pūṭṭik kaṭiteṟintār; vañcaṉuṭal
vaṇṇattik kaṇṭu vaḷikōtta kikaḷittam
kaṉṉattil ūṉṟiṉār; kaṇṇimippu nīkkiṉār;
eṉṉaṭi! āyi ! ivaṉumoru kaḷvaṉō!
cittirattum kāṇac ciṟanta vaṭivaḻakaṉ!
itkiya vīraṉ iḻitoḻilic ceyvāṉō!
pūraṇac cantiraṉip pōṉṟa mukamuṭiyāṉ!
māraṉum maṇṭiyiṭṭu maṉṟāṭum pēraḻakaṉ!
pālpōl veḷutta pallaḻakuk kāraṉivaṉ!
cēlpōl viḻiyuṭiyār cinti kuṭipukuvāṉ!
eṉṟu palarpēca iḷiyāḷ orumaṅki
naṉṟu pukaṉṟīrnīr nāṭṭu naṭappaṟiyīr!
teyvapattik kāṉa tiruvēṭam koṇṭavarkaḷ 
viyakattil pātakarāy vāḻuvatik kāṇīrō!
nāṭṭiṉ nalattukkē nāṅkaḷ uḻikkiṉṟōm!
kēṭṭi aḻikkumuṉ kīḻppaṭiyōm ! yārkkumeṉak
kūṭṭattil niṉṟu kutittup palapēci
vīṭṭukkuḷ vēṟupala ceytirukkum vīṇarinīr!
kaṇṭum aṟiyīrō! kēṭṭum uṇarīrō!
uṇṭō umatakattil uṇmi teriyumoḷi?
nalla marakatampōl kaṇkavarum nañciṉilē
kollum koṭumi kuṭiyiruttal kāṇīrō!
kaṇṇāṭi pōlak kaviḻntu mukampārkka
oṇṇum teḷinīriṉ uḷḷē palanañcup
pūccikaḷum vāḻntu pucippōri māykkiṉṟa
ācciriyam nīṅkaḷ aṟiyīrō! taṅkakiḷ!
taṅka vilimatippit tāḻvākac ceyyulakam 
aṅkataṉiṟ cērntirukkum ceyti aṟiyīrō!
vāca malarccōli vāḻntuvarum teṉṟaluṭaṉ
nāca viṭakkāṟṟuc cērntu naliyātō!
ātalāl nīṅkaḷ aḻakāl mayaṅkātīr!
mātarīr! coṉṉa maṭami moḻimaṟappīr!
eṉṉak kuyiṟkulattār uṇmiyivaḷ colleṉṟē
paṉṉi metuvākap pāviyarum ceṉṟārkaḷ.
tōḻarkaḷ ceyki
kūṭṭāḷik kaḷvar kuḻuviṉ naṭuviṉilē
kāṭṭāmal tammuruvik kaṇkalaṅkic ceṉṟārkaḷ;
taṅkaḷ talivaṉit tappivikkac cintittār
eṅkeṅkum kāvalarkaḷ eccarikki yāyccūḻntu 
celluvataṉā loṉṟum ceyya vaḻiyiṉṟi
mella naḻuviviṭṭār mikka tuyaruṭaṉē.

iḷavaraciyum kaḷvaṉum oruvariyoruvar kāṇutal
inta vakiyāka ellālōrum cūḻntuvara
antap peruvīraṉ taṉṉi araṇmaṉiyiṉ
vīti vaḻiyāka virintiḻuttu vantārkaḷ;
ōtum peruviḻāvil ōṅkum olipōṉṟa
makkaḷ kuralōci maṉṉaṉ makaḷkēṭṭāḷ;
ekkā raṇaminta ōcikku eṉaniṉintāḷ;
cēṭiyar kūṭṭamuṭaṉ cērntuviḷi yāṭiyavaḷ
ōṭiyē vantuṟṟāḷ uppariki mītiṉilē
kūṭṭatti nōkkiṉāḷ kōmaḷa valliyavaḷ
vāṭṭamilāt tōṟṟam! vaḷarntapiṟi pōṉṟamukam,
ārvap payirvaḷara āṇmi maḻipoḻiyum
mārpam uṭiyāṉ! maṉakkiṉiya ṉāyniṉṟāṉ!
kuṉṟaṉiya tōḷkaḷ ! kuṟiyākkem pīranaṭi
aṉṟalarnta tāmariyē aṉṉa tirukkaṇkaḷ!
uḷḷāṉ ikkaṭṭaḻakaṉ! ūrkkā valarkūṭik
kaḷḷaṉippōl kaṭṭi iḻukkiṉṟār ēṉōtāṉ?
eṉṟu palaveṇṇi imiyāmaṟ kaṇmalaric
ceṉṟu mukaṅkuḷirac cintiṉāḷ; cuntaraṉum
aṇṇāntu pārkka araṇmaṉiyiṉ māṭiyilē
taṇṇāram pūṇṭirunta tiyal oruttipala
viṇmīṉ puṭicūḻa veḷḷi nilāvīcit 
taṇmatiyam oṉṟu tavampurintu niṟpatupōl
kārikiyāḷ niṉṟutaṉ kaṇmalarkaḷ vīcuvati
vīraṉun tāṉkaṇṭāṉ viṭṭeṟintāṉ tāmariyic
centā mariyōṭi cevvaricūḻ vaṇṭukaḷip 
pintēṉ aruttutaṟkup paṟṟumoru ceykipōl
aṉṉattiṉ kaṇkaḷuṭaṉ aṉpāṟ kalantuviṭa
miṉṉal iṭiyāḷum, meymaṟantu niṉṟu viṭṭāḷ
naṉṟāy avaṉuruvi nāṭṭiviṭṭu neñciṉilē
aṉṟē avaṉmārpil aṉpip patittuviṭṭāḷ;
kātal perunōkkāl kaṭṭaḻakaṉ āṇmitaṉi
 mōti aḻittavaḷum mōka valivīḻntāḷ;
kaṇṇoṭu kaṇṇiṇi nōkkokkiṉ vāyccoṟkaḷ
eṉṉa payaṉum ila veṉṟu vaḷḷuvar
coṉṉa moḻipalikkac cuntaraṉum appoḻutē
tēṅkip perumūccu viṭṭāṉ; tikippuṟṟāḷ;
pāṅkirunta tōḻiyarum pārttār avaḷniliyi;
kūṭṭam maṟiyumaṭṭum kaṇṇimikaḷ koṭṭāmal
tīṭṭāta cittiramāy niṉṟāḷ terunōkki;
māntar tiraḷmaṟinta piṉṉar, matimukattuc
cāntaṇinta kūntalic calippuṟṟa pārviyāḷiṉ 
vāṭi maṉamcōr vaṭivamatit tōḻiyarkaḷ 
vēṭikki pēci viḷiyāṭak koṇṭaṇintār.
kaḷvaṉi aracaṉmuṉ koṇartal
peṟṟōri yaṉṟip piṟaporuḷkaḷ vēṇṭuvaṉa
muṟṟāka vāṅka muṭiyum kaṭitteruvum 
vāṉakattē yām peṟṟa vāḻvuḷavō eṉṟaṟiya 
āṉavari yōṅki aḻakirukkum māṭaṅkaḷ
mītē mukiliruntu mikkakuḷīr kāṭṭukiṉṟa 
vīti kaḷumkaṭantu vīram niṟintaperum
añcāta cuntara nātaṉ avaṉ taṉṉik
koñca  nērattiṟkuḷ koṇṭaṇintār maṉṉaṉmuṉ
maṉṉaṉ tayāniti makiḻvaṭintāṉ appōtu;
kaṉṉeñcac cuntaraṉik kaṭṭik koṇarntavarkkut
takka paricaḷittāṉ; tāḻāmal nīrivaṉi
ikkaṇamē koṇṭu ciṟikkuḷ iruttiṭuvīr!
nāḷip pakaṟpōtil nammuṭiya maṉṟattil 
nīḷa vicarittu nītimuṟi ceyvōm! 
eṉṉa moḻintaṉpiṉ ellōrum pōyviṭṭār.
maṇṇuḷōr neñcam kalaṅki mayakkamuṟap
paṇṇum perunōyip paṟṟip piṇittāṟpōl
aṉṉava ṉicciṟiyil taḷḷi aṭittār;
iravu viṭiyumaṭṭum kāvalarkaḷ ellām
uṟaṅkāmal kāttār oruṅku.

2
karukiya kañcam
añcukatti maṉṉaṉ aḷitta cuṭarviḷakki
neñca valippaṭutti niṉṟatoru cuntaraṉum
tūṅkāṉ viḻittiruntāṉ; tukkak kaṭalpaṭintāṉ;
āṅkē tāṉpārtta aḻakāṉa peṇmayili
eṇṇiyeṇṇi iṉpuṟuvāṉ; ēṅkuvāṉ neñcākum
kaṇṇiṉāṟ kaṇṭu kalaṅkuvāṉ; cintippāṉ;
kaḷvaṉ maṉakkātal
pārulakikal nāṉkaṇṭa pāviyarkaḷ paṟpalapēr
ārumeṉ neñciṉilē añcāmal vantatili;
ettaṉiyō maṅkimār eṉṉi maṇappataṟkuc
cittattil eṇṇiyeṉic cērnturitta kālamelām
caṟṟum umivirumpēṉ ! nāṉmaṇakkac cammatiyēṉ
muṟṟum veṟukkiṉṟēṉ! moykuḻalīr! eṉṟurittēṉ!
veppattiṟ paṭṭurukum veṇṇeyyip pōluḷḷam
ippaṭi ninturukac ceytāḷ, eḻilaraci!
uppariki mēlniṉ ṟorupūṅ koṭiyeṉmēl
oppariya nīla malari utirttuviṭṭāḷ!
annīlap pūvināṉ āvalāyk kaṇvaḻiyē
eṉṉeñciṉ uḷvittēṉ! iṉpuṟṟēṉ! eṉceyvēṉ!
kollumvilaṅku kuṭiyirukkum kāṭukaḷil
cellum poḻutumnāṉ cinti aḻintatili;
niṉṟukīḻ nōkkiṉ nilikalaṅkac ceykiṉṟa
kuṉṟiṉmēl ēṟik kutittapō tum añcēṉ!
veḷḷaṅ karipuraṇṭu vīḻap palamaraṅkaḷ 
taḷḷi aṇiyuṭittut  tāṅkāmal ōṭum 
natikaḷit tāṇṭutaṟkum nāṉ ciṟitum añcēṉ
putiyavaḻi muṭputarkkuḷ pōvēṉ tuṇivuṭaṉē!
kaṇkoṇṭu pārttāl kalakkamuṟum kāriruḷil
maṇmuḻutum cuṟṟi makiḻvēṉ! maṉaṅkalaṅkēṉ!
ciṅkam pulikaḷeṉic cīṟi etirttālum
aṅkē taṭuppēṉ! aḻippēṉ avimāḷa!
vīrāti vīrarelām vīḻntē taripuraḷat 
tīramuṭaṉ pōrceyvēṉ! tīrppēṉ avaruṭali!
uḷḷamum pēccum orutaṉmi yillāmal
kaḷḷat taṉañcey kayavarik kaṇṭālum
eḷḷat taṉikūṭa añcā(tu) etirttavarip
paḷḷattil vīḻum paṭiveṟṟi peṟṟiṭuvēṉ
ekkālum cōrāta taṉmiyuḷa eṉṉeñcam 
ikkālam vāṭi irukkum tiṟamaṟiyēṉ!
kaṉṉikiyiṉ vēlviḻiyum kaṭṭaḻakum cērnteṉṉi
inniliyil vittaṉavē āā! ituviyappē!
ā! veṉ matiyirunta vāṟeṉ? aṟivillēṉ! 
pāvi avaḷivvūrp pārttipaṉ celvitāṉ!
eṭṭāta māṅkaṉiyi eyta oruciṟuvaṉ
koṭṭāvi pōkkalpōl āṉēṉ! koṭiyaṉēṉ!
vāṉat tiḷampiṟiyi vāveṉ ṟaḻittaḻutu
kāṉal kuralkāṭṭum kikkuḻanti pōlāṉēṉ!
pañcavar ṇakkiḷiyip pārppataṟkuk kaṇṇillāṉ
koñcik koṭuveṉṟu kēṭpatāl eṉṉapayaṉ?
vāyillā maṅki vaḷarkītam kaṟpataṉāl
āya palaṉeṉṉa? aṅkōr malimukaṭṭil
centēṉ aṭipiḷantu cinta ativirumpi
nontutaṉ nānīṭṭum noṇṭi peṟumpayaṉeṉ?
inta vitamellām eṇṇi avaṉimippil
cinti paṟipōkac ceytirunta nērattē!
māṉēr viḻikkumari maṅki malaraṇintu
tāṉē avaṉetiril puṉmuṟuval tantirukka,
maṉṉaṉ makaḷvantāḷ eṉṟu maruṇṭavaṉāyc
coṉṉāṉ palamoḻikaḷ cōrntu
kaḷvaṉ kūṟutal 
cintu
neñcil kuṭipukuntāy - nili
niṉṟu kaṇkāṭṭi mayakkiṉi māṉē!
añcum viṉipurintēṉ! - iṉi
attoḻil ceytilēṉ! viṭṭaṉeṉ āṇi!
nañcāy eṉi varuttēl !- oru
naṉmoḻi tantuyir kāttaruḷ vāynī!
vañci yiṇiviḻiyāl - maṉa
vallami tīrntu maṟintatu mātē!
paṭṭiṉi vātiyiṉāl  - intap 
pāriṉil tuṉpam piṇikoṇṭu tavippār
kiṭṭiya nalluṇavāl - neñcam 
kiḷarntu makiḻntu kaḷippuṟal pōlē
kaṭṭik karumpuṉiyē - nāṉ
kaṇṭatum kātal koṇṭu makiḻntēṉ;
maṭṭit taṉamataṉāl - inta
mānila mītiṉi tītukaḷ ceyyēṉ;
kātal taṉiyitunāḷ - varik 
kaṇṭaṟiyēṉ; collak kēṭṭatēyaṉṟik,
kōti mārpalarāl - maṉam 
koḷḷi koḷḷāmaṟ piḻittaṉeṉ! innāḷ,
mātē! uṉataḻakāl -iṉṟu
maṉaṅkavar kātal ituveṉṟu kaṇṭēṉ!
ētō ituvaṟiyēṉ! nī
eṉuḷḷam koṇṭaṉi ! vīḻntaṉeṉ uṉpāl!
maṉṉaṉ maṭamakaḷē! - oḷir
matimukaṅ koṇṭeṉ maṉattuḷ nuḻintē
eṉṉip putumiyilē - nī
iruttiṉi pittaṉpōl āyiṉeṉ niṉṟēṉ
uṉṉāl kuṟiyaṟintēṉ - iṉi
oruvakit tīmiyum ceytiṭa māṭṭēṉ!
kaṇṇē! iṉi varuttēl! - eṉṟaṉ
kiyil aṇintaruḷ! kaṇkavar tēvi!
(kaliveṇpā)
kaḷḷaṉiv vāṟurittuk kārikiyik kippiṭikka
meḷḷa veḻuttiruntāṉ; miṉṉaṟ koṭimaṟintāḷ;
uḷḷattē uṇmi uṇarāta tālavaṉum 
tuḷḷik kutittāṉ; tuḻāviṉāṉ aṅkellām;
uḷḷattāl eṇṇum uruvam kaṉaviṉilē
meḷḷa veḷippaṭum; meymi avaṉaṟiyāṉ
aṉṉāṉ maṉattuḷḷē vāḻnta avaḷuruvē
kaṇṇiṉ etirtōṉṟik kāṭci aḷittateṉak
kaṇṭaṟi yāmal katikaḷ palacollit 
tiṇṭāṭi niṉṟāḷ tiruṭaṉ; atukāli,
kāvalar kaṭital
kāvalarkaḷ aṉṉavaṉik kaṇṭu viyappuṟṟār;
āvā! ituveṉ ! aṟiyōm! uḷaṟal!
ciṟiyuḷ irukkumivaṉ cinti kalaṅki 
aṟivāṉ! ētētō; aiyamili pittiyamē!
eṉṟavarkaḷ pēci ēṇṭā uḷaṟukiṟāy!
naṉṟaṟiyāk kaḷvā! pitaṟṟātē! naṉṟāy
uṟaṅkuvāy ! nāḷi uyirkavar vāṉmaṉṉaṉ
maṟintu viṭumuṉṟaṉ maṉattuyaram eṉṟārkaḷ


kaḷvaṉ viṭi
maṉṉavaṉ peṇpāl maṉatti aṭakuvitta
aṉṉavaṉ,pētikāḷ! āynteṉ niliyaṟiyīr!
pittaṉeṉ ṟētētō pēci irāmuḻutum
antac ciṟikkuḷ iruntārkaḷ. avviravil
neñcam paṟikoṭutta nēriḻiyāḷ paṭṭatuyar
koñcamala vellām kūṟuvataṟ kārvallār?
aracakumāriyiṉ tuṉpam
nīlat tukilvirittu nittilaṅkaḷ mēḷparappik
kōlañcey veḷḷik kuṭamoṉṟu vittatupōl
ūṭalilē cōrntirunta kātalarkaḷ uḷḷamalar
kūṭalilē cellak kumutam itaḻviriya,
veḷḷi nilāccinti, veṅkatirāl tuṉpuṟṟōr
uḷḷamakiḻ veyta utittāṉ mativāṉil;
vanta matikaṇṭu vāḻttiṉār kātalarkaḷ;
inta nilavamuti ellōrum uṇṭiruntār;
kaḷvaṉiṭam neñcu paṟipōṉa kārikitāṉ
oḷvaḷikaḷ cōra, uṭampellām tīccūḻa,
nāṭṭiyam ceypatumi nāṇaṟṟu vīḻntateṉa
āṭṭa melāmviṭṭāḷ; amaḷiyiṉ mēṟkiṭantāḷ;
kāykkumoru māṅkaṉṟik kattiyiṉāl veṭṭic
cāytta teṉakkiṭantāḷ; cāmpiṉāḷ; tēmpiṉāḷ;
nalla malarkkoṭiyi nācamuṟa vērōṭu
kallik kaṭuveyiliṟ kāya viṭuttateṉa
mulli muṟuvalāḷ cōrntāḷ mukamalartāṉ;
kollimāṉ neñcam kumuṟiṉāḷ; vāṭiṉāḷ;
kañca malaroṉṟu karukik kiṭantateṉap 
pañcaṇiyil vīḻntu paṭuttiruntāḷ; pāvinili
kaṇṭatuṇit tōḻimār kaṇmalarkaḷ; mūṭavili;
uṇṭāḷō pōti! uṇarcci maṟantateṉ?
ētō mayakkuṟṟāḷ; collāḷ emakkoṉṟum;
tītō ivaḷuṭaṟkē! tēṟōm! eṉappulampik
kollaṉulikkāṟṟēpōl kōtimār mūccuyirttār;
alli malarkkoṭiyi āṟṟāta veyyililē
kiḷḷi yeṟiyak kiṭantu tuvaḷvatupōl
koḷḷic cirippuṭiya kovvi yitaḻāḷum
cērnta paṭukkiyilē cinti kalaṅkimukam 
cōrntu kiṭantāḷ cuḻaṉṟu.

ācciriyap pēriruḷ
( kaliveṇpā)
nīlaniṟak kampaḷiyi nīṇilattiṉ mēlvirittuk 
kōlap pacumpoṉ kuṭamniṟutti vittatupōl
kālik katirōṉ kaṭalmukattil tōṉṟumuṉ,
cōlip paṟaviyelām cōrviṉṟik kokkarittuk 
kūṭik kaḷittuk kutūkala māyiruppap
pāṭum paṟaviyiṉam pāṭi makiḻntirukka,
vaṉṉac ciṟikkulaṅkaḷ vārip pucippataṟkē
aṉṉatti yeṅkum aḷḷi yiṟittāṟpōl
mulli malarum muṟukkaviḻnta mallikiyum 
elliyilā vācaṉiyō ṭeṅkum malarntirukka,
koṇṭāṉ kotipṭintu kūṟum moḻittīyāl
cuṇṭum maṉivimukam pōlavē cōrvāki
alli malarkuviya; aṉpāyp palapēcip
pulla varumkoḻunaṉ puṉṉakiyāl neñciṉilē
tuḷḷum makiḻccimukam tōṉṟuvatu pōlmaṉatti
aḷḷum taṭantōṟum tāmarikaḷ vāyaviḻap;
paḷḷic ciṟuvarelām pāṭam paṭippataṟkut
tuḷḷi yeḻuntirun tuṭkārntu tūṅkiviḻa
vēlikkuc celvōr virinteḻuntu tattamatu
kālik kaṭaṉkaḷ kaṭitākac ceytiruppat
tūkkam viḻitteḻuntu tōkiyarkaḷ ellōrum
ūkkatti āṭavarum kūṭṭum mukantirutti
illattu vēlikaḷic ceytirukkum elliyilē,
ellōrum kāṇa iḷampariti tōṉṟiṉāṉ.
poṅkum neruppāyp polikiṉṟa cūriyaṉum
eṅkeṅkum taṉṉoḷiyi ēṟap parappiṉāṉ.
avvoḷiyiṉ veḷḷattil āḻnta poruḷellām
cevviyāyp poṉcālvi pōrttic ciṟantaṉavē!
tōṭṭa malarkaḷ toṭiyaviḻntu vaṇṭukaḷiṉ
kūṭṭaṅ kaḷikkūvit tēṉaruttik koṇṭirukkum;
innērap pōtilav vūril iruppavarkaḷ
muṉṉāḷ nikaḻcci mutalil niṉittārkaḷ;
añcāta ciṅkaṉ vaḻakki namataracaṉ
cuntara nātaṉeṉum cōraṉ peruvaḻakki
intanāḷ nammaracaṉ kēṭpāṉ, koliceyvāṉ
iññāṉṟu kēṭpāṉ; iṭuvāṉ kolittaṇṭam;
nāmuṉṉē ceṉṟanta naṉṉikaḻcci kāṇuvōm!
pōmeṉṟu yārnammit taḷḷiṉum pōkōm!
eṉappēci māntarellām ērāḷa māka
vaṉappāṉa maṉṉaṉ capinōkki vantiruntār.
kaḷvaṉ vaḻakku
karantinakar vēntaṉ kalaṅkāta neñcaṉ
aṟantavaṟā malneṟiyil āḷum aṟivuṭiyōṉ
aṉṉāṉ orucelvam āṭcik kuriyamakaḷ
poṉṉē eṉakkaṇṭār pōṟṟum eḻiṟkoḻuntu
kōmaḷa valliyeṉak kūṟum peyaruṭiyāḷ,
kāmattip pāyccum kaṇṇuṭiyāḷ, cevvitaḻāḷ;
nīril nimirntu niṟkum malarmukattāḷ;
pārap peruñcuvicēr pintamiḻiṉ cēmaniti;
nūlpalavum kaṟṟaṟintāḷ; nuṭpa matiyuṭiyāḷ;
pālpōṉṟa veṇmaṉattāḷ; pāviṉ cuvi nukarvāḷ;
nītinūṟ koḷki yelām naṉkaṟivāḷ, nīḷkuḻalāḷ;
ōtuvatiṟ kālatti ōyāmaṟ pōkkuvāḷ.
aṉṉavaḷ pālaracaṉ ārā viruppuṭiyaṉ;
eṉṉaceya lēṉum ivaḷiṭattiṟ collāmal
ceyvatili ātālāl, centamiḻk kaṉṉiyavaḷ
vikum iṭantēṭi vantu muṭimaṉṉaṉ,
mātar kulakkoḻuntē! nāḷinam mācapiyil
tītupala ōruruvāyt tiraṇṭeḻun tāṟpōṉṟōṉ
cūtilē vallavaṉ cuntara nātaṉeṉum 
kātakaṉ kaḷvaṉ kaṭuñcinti yāḷaṉmēl
vaḻakku naṭakkum vakikāṇa nīyum 
paḻakka muḷatōḻi yuṭaṉvantu pārttiruppāy!
paṭṭat tiḷavaraci yātalāṟ pārāḷum 
caṭṭatiṭ ṭaṅkaḷ cariyākak kaṇṭuṇarvāy!
eṉṟurittāṉ. maṉṉavaṉum ēṟṟa uṭipūṇṭu
maṉṟam pukuntāṉ; matiyamiccar pōṟṟiṉār;
ūrārkaḷ ellōrum uḷḷak kaḷippuṭaṉē
pārāḷum vēntaṉi vāḻttip paṇintārkaḷ;
ārā amutaṉiyāḷ aṅkōr puṟattamarntu
māraṉuk kōli celaviṭṭu makiḻntiruntāḷ.
kāvalarkaḷ avviya kaḷvaṉik kaṭṭuṭaṉē,
āvaluṭaṉ makkaḷ aṉivōrum pārkka
neruṅki yiṭittu viḻipituṅki niṟkak,
karuṅkal maṉakkaḷvaṉ taṉṉik, kalimaṉṉaṉ
maṉṟattē yīrttuk koṇarntu, maṉañcaliyā
teṉṟum paṇipurivōr vittār, etirniṟutti.
appoḻutu maṉṉaṉ avaṉmuṉ purintapala
tappitaṅkaḷ kāṭṭuvāṉ tāṉ.
aracaṉ kūṟutul
(āciriya viruttam)
ūrāriṉ maṉampatikkat tīmi ceytāy!
	oruvarukkum teriyamal oḷinti runtāy!
ērāḷa māyuḻittu varunti nāḷum 
	iṉpuṟavē makkaḷelām celvam cērppār;
ōrāmal avikaḷinī tiruṭic ceṉṟē
	uṉcukattip pēṇutalōr kuṟṟam aṉṟē?
cīrāṉa naṉṉeṟikaḷ kaṭippi ṭittē
	ciṟantavaṉāy vāḻumaṟi vuṉakkēṉ illi?
pāṭupaṭṭiṅ kuḻippataṟkē kāluṅ kiyum
	palamāṉa kaṭṭuṭalum peṟṟi ruppāy!
nāṭuviṭṭu nāṭōṭip palaru ḻittē
	nalvāḻvu naṭattuvatum kaṇṭu niṉṟāy !
kāṭutaṉil viṟakoṭittu viṟṟu vāḻiṉ 
	kaṇṭavarkaḷ uṉiyikaḻār aṉṟō colka!
tēṭutaṟku muṭiyāta māṉan taṉṉit 
	tiruṭaṉeṉum paḻipūṇṭu viṭṭu viṭṭāy!
potumakkaḷ matikkumoru toḻilic ceytu
	pukaḻāka vāḻvataṟkām aṟivē yiṉṟip
patitarkaḷiṉ kūṭṭattit tuṇiyāk koṇṭāy!
	pālarkaḷum mātarkaḷum naṭukkam koḷḷa
atikoṭiya ceyalkaḷilē īṭu paṭṭāy!
	aḻakiyavuṉ kaṭṭiḷami aḻittu viṭṭāy!
matuvarunti matikeṭṭut tirintu niṉṟāy!
	maṉitaṉeṉa uṉiyiṅku matippār uṇṭō!
kaḷḷaviṉit toḻilpurintu vāḻntu vantāy
	kayavaṉē! ivvaḷavō uṉatu kuṟṟam !
meḷḷevar coṉṉālum accol kēṭpiṉ
	mēṉiyelām vitirvitirttu vērvi yāki
uḷḷamelām cōrvaṭiyum koliyic ceyya
	orupoḻutum añcāta valiya neñcā!
koḷḷiyuyir koṉṟulaka kāla ṉākik
	kolipputariṉ uḷvāḻnta koṭiya tīyā!
piṭittuṉṉik koṇṭuvarak kāval vīrar 
	pirintupala ticikaḷilum tēṭip pārttār;
taṭittaṉattāl avarkiyil akappaṭāmal
	tappittu maṟivākat tirintu vāḻntāy!
kuṭittaṉattār palaruṉṉāl tuṉpam eyta
	koṭuṅkōlkoḷ maṉṉavaṉāyk kuṟumpu ceytāy!
aṭittiṅku viraṭṭiviṭa māṭṭēṉ uṉṟaṉ
	aṭātapaḻik kēṟṟavoru taṇṭam ceyvēṉ!  
kaḷvaṉ vēṇṭukōḷum
aracaṉ viṭiyum 
(paḵṟoṭi veṇpā)
vēntaṉ urikēṭṭu vīṟṟirunta mantirimār,
māntar palarumoru māṟṟamuriyā tiruntār.
añcāta ciṅkaṉ aracaṉmuka nōkkik
cōrā mukamuṭiya cuntaranātaṉ avaṉ
nērē nimirntaṅku niṉṟu moḻintiṭṭāṉ;
koñcamum cōrvilāṉ kūṟat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ;
pūmaṇ ṭalamāḷum vēntē! pukaḻuṭiyōy!
yāmumoru viṇṇappam ceyvōm! emakkunī
uttaravu tantāl urikkiṉṟōm iccapiyiṉ 
mattiyilē eṉṟavaṉ māṟṟumoḻi kūṟiṉāṉ;
evvurikaḷ kūṟanī eṇṇiṭiṉum ippoḻutē
avvurikaḷ collalām añcāmal eṉṟanta
maṉṉaṉ viṭitantāṉ; māvīraṉ cuntaraṉum
coṉṉāṉ palamoḻikaḷ cūḻntu.
kaḷvaṉ moḻi
eṉṉiyoru tiruṭaṉeṉa muṭivu ceytāy,
	iḻivākap palamoḻikaḷ pēci viṭṭāy!
aṉṉiyiṉun tayavuṭiyāṉ nīyeṉ ṟeṇṇi
	aṟiyātār uṉpātam tāṅki niṉṟār!
uṉṉiyumnāṉ tiruṭaṉeṉak kūṟu kiṉṟēṉ!
	uṇmi yeṉṟē meyppippēṉ uṟutiyāka!
maṉṉippāy eṉṉuriyi aracar ēṟē!
	maṭamiyiṉāl aḻivaḻakkuc coṉṉēṉ allēṉ!
kolikāraṉ eṉaveṉṉi ikaḻntu māntar
	kūṭṭamelām parikacikka vacimo ḻintāy!
niliyāka eṉatāvi kāppa taṟkē
	niṉṟeṉiyār etirtālum aḻitti ruppēṉ!
alicūḻum kaṭalulakil maṉṉar tammāl
	āruyirkaḷ aḻivataṟkōr aḷavum uṇṭō!
maliyāmal eṉmoḻiyi eṇṇip pārnī!
	maṉṉavaṉē koliñaṉnāṉ oruvaṉ tāṉō?
aracaṉ ciṉam
ivvurikaḷ cuntara nātaṉ iyampiyapiṉ 
kovviyeṉak kaṇcivantu kōpam kuṭikoḷḷa
mīci tuṭituṭikka, mēluntaṉ mārpiṉilē
vīciyapal kūrampu pāyntāl eṉavekuṇṭu,
vēntaṉ eḻuntiruntu viṇṇatirak kūviṉāṉ;
cāntatti māyttāṉ; caṭacaṭaṭeṉap pecukiṉṟāṉ;
ēṭā! kolikkaḷvā! uṉṉāṇmi evvaḷavu?
tēṭāp perumpaḻikkuṉ tēkam paṟikoṭuttāy!
nāṭṭukku maṉṉavaṉi nākkup puraḷumaṭṭum
kēṭṭukkuḷ āṉamoḻi pēciṉāy kīḻmakaṉē!
tēcak kuṭimakkaḷ tēmpit tiriyāmal 
vācam purivataṟkē vāḻum aracaṉiṭam
nēcattik kāṭṭāmal nītāṉ paḻittāy ! uṉ
mōcatti ūrār muḻutum aṟivār kaḷ ! 
eṉṉiyumuṉ tōḻaṉeṉak kūṟi etirmoḻintāy
uṉṉit talivīḻtti ōṭṭukiṟēṉ pār! eṉṟu
maṉṉaṉ kaṭukaṭuttāṉ; maṉṟattār accamuṟat 
taṉṉuṭiya vāṭpaṭiyik kikkoṇṭu tāṇṭiṉāṉ;
maṉṟattār ellōrum maṇpatumi yāṉārkaḷ;
ceṉṟōṭi ṉāṉmaṉṉaṉ; ceṅkarattu vāḷpaṟṟi,
mantiri āymatiyaṉ maṉṟāṭik kūṟukiṉṟāṉ,
centamiḻ vallaṉē! cīṟṟam taṇintiṭuvāy!
ippō tivaṉkūṟum īṉap poruḷmoḻikaḷ
eppaṭittāṉ okkumeṉak kēṭpōm ivaṉiyē!
eṉṟu moḻintavuṭaṉ vāḷkoṇṭa ēntaluntāṉ 
niṉṟāṉ ciṉantaṇintu, nīrviṭṭa tīyāki;
āṇṭirunta kaḷvaṉ añcāmukam nōkki,
ēṇṭā! talikkiṟukkā! ippōtu nīcoṉṉa
vīṇmoḻiyi naṉṟāy viḷakki urittāṟpiṉ
kāṇkiṉṟēṉ uṉṉuṭiya kivarici yāvu meṉa
maṉṉaṉ ciṉantinta māṟṟam pukaṉṟavuṭaṉ,
kaṉṉeñcaṉ colvāṉ kaṭintu.
kaḷvaṉ uri
tēcattār tuyarkaḷiya vāḻvēṉ eṉṟu
	terivitta malarmārpiṉ maṉṉā kēḷnī !
mācaṟṟa maṉamuṭiyār ēḻi makkaḷ
	mānilatti vaḷappaṭutti viḷiya vippār!
vācamalarp pūñcōli vaḷarttu vippār!
	vāykkālkaḷ āṟukaḷum akaḻntu vippār!
nēcattāl nīnalaṅkaḷ ceyvā yeṉṟu
	niṉittiruntār aṟiyāta taṉmi yālē!
irattamaḻi vērviyeṉa nilattē cinti
	ennāḷum pāṭupaṭum makka ḷālē
peruttapala nakaraṅkaḷ, terukkaḷ, vīṭu,
	piḻittiruntu vāḻvataṟkām uṇavup paṇṭam
turittaṉatti naṭattutaṟkuc celva mellām
	tōṉṟivarum uṇmiyitu maṟukka lāmō!
varuttiyavar tamiccuraṇṭu vōri nīṅkaḷ
	vāḻvikka ! virukkiṉṟīr payaṉum uṇṭō!
pakaliravu pārāmal uṭali vāṭṭip
	palatoḻilum purintulakil vāḻum makkaḷ
mikavarunti iṭamillā tuṇavum iṉṟi
	melintaḻintē patikkiṉṟār; utavi yilli;
cakamellām pañcameṉum koṭumpēy cūḻntu
	calipṭiya makkaḷ tamik kacakkiṉālum
takaviṉṟi varivāṅki aracu ceytal
	tarumamō! nītivaḻi naṭappa tāmō!
ennāḷum varikaḷiṉmēl varikaḷ pōṭṭē
	ēḻikaḷiṉ viḻikalaṅkak koṭumi ceytal
maṉṉānaṟ ceykiyō! nīti tāṉō!
	matiyuḷḷār ceyalmuyaṟṟum aṟamē tāmō!
uṉṉāṇi urikkiṉṟēṉ, āyntu pār nī !
	oruvitattum pētamili nāṉum nīyum!
coṉṉālnī pakaṟkoḷḷi aṭittu niṟpāy!
	cōraṉ irāk kālattil tiruṭi niṉṟēṉ!
kolikāraṉ eṉṟeṉṉi ikaḻntē coṉṉāy!
	kūṟukiṟēṉ uṉkoli yikkuṟittuk kēḷnī!
niliyāyim maṇṇulaki āḷum kātal
	niṉippōṭu paṭikaḷ pala cērntu vittāy!
caliyāmal pōrceytu talikaḷ cāyppāy!
	caḷacaḷeṉa irattanati ōṭac ceyvāy!
uliyāta vīraṉeṉum peyari nāṭi
	uṭaltuṭikka uyirkoḷvāy nīyum aṉṟō!
ātaliṉāl maṉṉavaṉē! nīyum nāṉum
	āṟṟuvatu vēṟutoḻil eṉṉa veṇṇēl;
pētamili iruvarukkuḷ eṇṇip pārttāl;
	pittuṭiya māntariti uṇara māṭṭār;
cātiyilē uyarvurittut tāḻvum collic
	catipurinta tāliṅkē mayaṅki niṉṟāy!
nītiyilē pakuttaṟivi vittu nōkkiṉ
	nicanteriyum eṉmoḻiyi niṉittup pārnī!
amiccaṉ moḻital
(āciriyappā)
añcāta ciṅkaṉ aṟinta moḻikaḷ
neñciṟ pukuntu nañcāy kalakka,
maṉṉaṉ ciṉattāl taṉṉaṟi viḻantāṉ;
eṉṉa viṭitāṉiṟuppateṉ ṟeṇṇāṉ;
pērava māṉap pilattuḷ vīḻntāṉ,
āravaṉ? maṭappayal! īramil neñcaṉ!
ulaka nīti oṉṟum uṇarāṉ!
palapala uḷaṟiṉaṉ pakuttaṟi viṉṟi!
maṉṉavaṉ iṉṟēl māṉiṭac cōli
taṉṉiṟ kalakac cuḻaltāṉ pukuntu 
cāntam kulikkum; caṭṭatiṭ ṭaṅkaḷ 
māntar aṟiyār! mākkaḷ pōṉṟē
varituṟi yillā vāḻkkiyiṟ cikkik 
kariyilāk kaṇvāy nīreṉap pōvar;
innili nērā tirukkum poruṭṭē
maṉṉaṉ varikaḷ vāṅki vāṅki avaṟṟi
āḷukip pukiratam āpat tiṉṟi
nāḷum naṉku naṭipeṟa nalkuvaṉ;
ariya amiti muṟiyum paṭiyē
veṟināy ākik kalakam viḷippōrc
citaṟa aṭippāṉ cēṉikaḷ koṇṭē;
itileṉ kuṟṟam? iyamputi pētāy!
eṉṟāy matiyaṉ niṉṟu moḻintiṭa,
oṉṟum uriyāṉ akkoṭuṅ kaḷvaṉ.
naṉṟu naṉṟim mantiri cūṭeṉa
maṉṟuḷōr makiḻntuki koṭṭa,
niṉṟa kaḷvaṉum nōkkiṉaṉ nimirntē.
kaḷvaṉ viṭi
matiniṟinta mantiriyār patilik kēṭṭīr!
	makiḻcciyuṭaṉ karaṅkoṭṭi ārppa rittīr!
putumiyatil ētumilli; paḻiya vārtti;
	pukalkiṉṟēṉ nāṉumitō uṇmi kēḷīr!
katiyaṟiyār piḻippataṟkum, kāla mellām
	kalakkaṅkoḷ maṉitarpalar eṉṉē ṭuḷḷār;
ativaṟiyar! paritāpar! avarik kāttal
	aṭiyēṟkum kaṭaṉākum kāttu niṉṟēṉ!
eṉṉuṭiya kuṭipaṭikaḷ piḻippataṟkē
	eppoḻutum celvar poruḷ koḷḷi koṇṭēṉ;
maṇṇulakil ennāḷum vaṟiyar celvam
	maṉatārat tiruṭiṉatē illi! illi!
kaṉṉamiṭuṅ kāleṉṉip piṭittu yirtāṉ
	kavaravaru vōrināṉ koṉṟa tuṇṭu!
eṇṇuvēṉ tiruṭalkoli kuṟṟam eṉṟē
	eṉṉiṉum piṟarkkutavi karutic ceyvēṉ!
amiccaṉ urittal
(āciriyappā)
amiccaṉ ivvuri aṟintaṉaṉ maṉattil
imippil maṟumoḻi iyampak karutik
kaḷiyaṉi nōkkik kaṉittut 
teḷivil immoḻi terivit taṉaṉē!
(āciriya viruttam)
nīmoḻinta urimuḻutum kiḷaṟip pārttāl
	niliniṟkum pakuttaṟivu maṉṟan taṉṉil;
āmitilōr aiyamili, uṇmi! uṇmi!
	āṉālum aṟiñarkaḷāl vakuttu vittup
pūmitaṉil vaḻaṅkivarum kalikaḷ oṉṟum 
	pukalavili uṉtoḻilum muṟitāṉeṉṟu;
cēmamili uṉaturiyāl; payaṉu milli;
	ciṟuvayatil peruṅkuṟṟam ceytu niṉṟāy!
ariyaṉukkuk kaṭamiyeṉa vititta vaṟṟi
	avaṉpuriva taṟamaṉṟō! pāvam āmō!
ciṟiyēṉō! vaḻakkēṉō! taṇṭa mēṉō!
	cintippāy! iviyulaka naṉmik kaṉṟō!
kuṟicolli aracumuṟi paḻittu niṉṟu
	koṭuvaḻakkup pēcum nī turōki yāvāy!
tariyiliṉi uyirvāḻat takuti yaṟṟāy!
	tantiṭuvōm uṉakkiṉṟu maraṇat tīrppē!
aracaṉatu ceyalmuṟṟum nīti yeṉṟē
	amintuḷḷa kāraṇattāl acca miṉṟi 
muracaṟintu potumakkaḷ kāṇum vaṇṇam
	moḻintumuṟi ceykiṉṟāṉ veḷip paṭiyāy!
irakaciyattil vittuṉṟaṉ toḻili yeṉṟum 
	iravutaṉil maṟivākac ceyti yaṉṟō!
uriceyvāy ! ituvoṉṟē uṉatu ceyki
	ovvāta teṉpataṉik kāṭṭum aṉṟō!
aviyōr kūṟal 
(kaliveṇpā)
mantiriyiṉ poṉṉuriyāl maṉṉaṉ makiḻvaṭintāṉ
antac capiyōrum āḻntār peruṅkaḷippil
cuntara nātaṉum cōrntāṉ uriyiḻantāṉ
enta moḻiyum iyampā talikuṉintāṉ
maṉṉaṉ tayānitiyum mācapiyi nōkkiṉāṉ.
iṉṉāṉ vaḻakkaṟittīr taṇṭaṉi tāṉeṉṉa
vitikkalām ipperiya vīṇaṉukku? nīṅkaḷ
kotippiṉṟi naṉṟāyk kuṟittaṟintu kūṟuvīr!
eṉṟavuṭaṉ maṉṟattār ellōrum ōrkuralāyk 
koṉṟē yaḻittiṭuvīr! koṉṟē yaḻittiṭuvīr!
takkatit taṇṭaṉiyē! caṟṟun tayaṅkātīr!
ikkaṇamē ceytiṭuvīr! eṉṟu pukaṉṟārkaḷ.
antavitat taṇṭaṉiyē āṟṟat takumeṉṟu
mantirimār ellōrum maṉṉaṉiṭam coṉṉārkaḷ;
kuṟṟam puripavarmēl kōpap paṭuvatuvē
muṟṟum potumakkaḷ taṉmiyām, mūrkkaṉāy
ittariyil ēṉcilar ākiṉṟār eṉṟeṇṇi
attakiya kāraṇaṅkaḷ tammi aḻittuviṭiṉ,
ellōrum ivvulakil iṉpam palaveyti
nalla camūkamāy vāḻntu nalampeṟalām
eṉṟa pakuttaṟivut taṉmiyavark kiṉmiyāl 
koṉṟuviṭa vēṇṭumeṉak kūṟiviṭṭārtīrppē.
veṟṟinātaṉ kūṟutal
veṟṟinā taṉeṉpōṉ vivēkam mikavuṭiyōṉ
caṟṟum taḷarāmal cāṟṟukiṟāṉ maṉṉaṉpāl;
vēntē palavaṇakkam! veṟṟiyōṭu vāḻkanī!
māntarel lāmivaṉi māyttuviṭak kūṟiṉār;
kuṟṟattiṟ kēṟpak koṭuntaṇṭam nī vitittal
muṟṟum muṟitāṉ; moḻivataṟkum oṉṟumilli;
āyiṉum vīraṉ; aṟivuḷḷa kaṭṭiḷiñaṉ!
tīyaṉ ivaṉit tiruttutaṟkut takkatoru 
taṇṭaṉiyit tantu tavaṟiṉimēṟ ceyyāmal
kaṇṭippa tēnalla kāriyam eṉṟurittāṉ.


kaḷvaṉit taṇṭittal
iccollik kēṭṭavarkaḷ ellōrum kikoṭṭic 
cīccī! ivaṉ pittaṉ! eṉṟu cirittārkaḷ;
maṉṉaṉ itu coṉṉa mantiriyiṉ naṉmoḻiyi 
eṉṉaveṉak kēḷāṉ ikaḻntāṉ; ciṉamuṭaṉē,
kāḷip payalivaṉik kāvalil iṉṟuvittu
nāḷi naṭuppakalil nammūrk kolikkaḷattil
vittu niṟuttiyē vāḷāl ivaṉtaliyic
cettu viḻaveṭṭic citippīr! eṉatāṇi!
ittīrppuk kūṟi eḻuntāṉ muṭi maṉṉaṉ.
kaḷvaṉum araciyum
attīya kaḷvaṉ amutameṉa vīṟṟirunta 
īramalark kūntal ēntiḻiyi nōkkiṉāṉ;
nāri varavēṟṟāḷ; nāṇit talikuṉintāḷ;
kaṇṇiruntu muttuk kalakaleṉat cintiṉāḷ;
eṇṇamelām pāḻāki ēṅkip patipatittāḷ;
maṉṉavaṉō iṉṟu maraṇameṉat tīrppaḷittāṉ;
eṉṉaviṉic ceyvōm eṉṟu niṉikkumuṉ,
antōnam vāḻnāḷ aṉittum payaṉiṉṟi
intavitam āyiṟṟē eṉṟeṇṇik kaḷvaṉum
mūccaṭaṅkik kīḻē viḻuvataṟku muṉṉaṅkē 
pēcciṉṟip pārttirunta peṇṇaraci ōlamiṭṭu
vīḻntāḷ; atu kaṇṭār vēntumuta lāṉavarkaḷ;
āḻntārkaḷ appoḻutē ācciriyap pēriruḷil.
pāṅkirunta tātimār pāviyiṉit tāḻkkāmal
āṅkiruntu tūkki aṭintār araṇmaṉiyi.
vēntaṉ patittāṉ; virinteḻuntu pōyviṭṭāṉ;
māntarum ceṉṟār maruṇṭu.

pālivaṉa maḻi
(kaliveṇpā)
añcāta cuntaraṉ āvi paṟikkaveṉat
tañci nakarmaṉṉaṉ tantakoṭun taṇṭaṉiyāl
koñcum kiḷimoḻiyāḷ kōmaḷa valliyavaḷ
neñcaṅ kalaṅkiṉāḷ; nīrāl naṉintu viṭṭāḷ;
tāṅkāta tuṉpam talikkoṇṭāḷ. tēṉmalari
nīṅkāta vaṇṭiṉampōl niṉṟirunta tōḻimār,
paṟpalavām meyppaṇikaḷ pāṅkiruntu ceytārkaḷ;
coṟpamum naṉmiyili; cōrvoṉṟē kaṇṭapalaṉ!
maṉṉaṉ tāyanitiyum pārttāṉ makaḷniliyi;
aṉṉi taṇ kōtiyum kaṇṭāḷ; akam uṭintāḷ!
aṉṟupakaṟ pōtum, aṭuttavirāp pōtiṉilum 
naṉṟaṟinta paṇṭitarkaḷ nāṭi piṭittaṟintār;
metta vuyarmaruntu mēṉmēlum ūṭṭiviṭṭār;
attaṉiyum vīṇē! avaḷnōy kuṟiyavilli.
kaḷḷaṉik koṉṟuviṭum kālam malarntavuṭaṉ
koḷḷic cirippuṭiya kōmakaḷtaṉ nāṇaḻintāḷ;
uḷḷattiṉ uḷḷē uṟintirunta kātaliṉi
meḷḷa veḷiyiṭṭāḷ; mikka tuyaruṟṟāḷ;
maṅki maṉamaṟinta maṉṉavaṉun tēviyum 
aṅkirunta tōḻiyarum āveṉ ṟalaṟiviṭṭār;
ciccī! iḷaṅkuyilē! ciṉṉat taṉamniṉintāy!
paccik kuḻantipōl pārttu viḻipatittāy!
ūrār itiyuṇarntāl ōṅki nakiyārō?
pārilyār ittakiya paḷḷattil vīḻvārkaḷ?
tañci nakarmaṉṉaṉ tanta kulakkoḻuntu
pañcit tiruṭaṉittāṉ pārttu matiyiḻantāḷ;
kātalittāḷ! eṉṟē katipēci eppōtum 
pūtalattil uḷḷōruṉ puṉceyali eḷḷārō!
eṉṉa ceyalniṉittāy! eṉṉarumik kaṇmaṇiyē!
maṉṉar vaḻivanta māmayilē! inniṉippi
muṟṟum maṟantuviṭu! mōkam citintuviṭum!
caṟṟum paḻipaṭarā teṉṟaracaṉ cāṟṟiṉāṉ.
kōmaḷavalli kūṟutal
kiyāl tirukiyatum kaṭṭuṇṭa taṇṇīrip 
peyyum kuḻāypōlap peṇṇaḻutu taṉkiyik
kaṇkaḷilē vittuk kacakkiyatum tāriyāy
maṇmītu nīrcinti niṉṟu maṉaṅkuḻintāḷ;
veyyiṟ puḻuvāki vempiṉāḷ; mōkattāl
niyum maṉa muṭiyāḷ nāvaṭakkam viṭṭurippāḷ;
nīrpukuntu pāluḷḷē niṉṟu maṟintatupōl
vīraṉavaṉ eṉṉuḷ virivāyk kalantuviṭṭāṉ;
veṇṇilavil taṇmipōla vēṟupā ṭiṉṟiyeṉ
kaṇṇiṉ vaḻippukuntāṉ kaṭṭaḻakaṉ; taṅkiviṭṭāṉ.
pūttamalar vīcum putumaṇattip pōlavaṉik 
kōtta aḻakeṉṉik koḷḷikoṇṭu viṭṭatuvē!
paṇṭinūl kallāṉ pakuttaṟivuk kāraṉināṉ
kaṇṭuviṭṭa pōtiṉilē kātal valivīḻntēṉ!
kāraṇantāṉ ētoṉṟum kaṇṭaṟiyēṉ nāṉitaṟkē!
āreṉṟaṉ āvitaṉik kāppār avaṉaṉṟi!
eṉṟu tuṭituṭittāḷ; ēṅkiṉāḷ; tēmpiṉāḷ
taṉṟaṉi māmakaṭkut tārvēntaṉ colkiṉṟāṉ;
aracaṉ uri
kāviyaṅkaḷ kaṟṟatuvum, kaṟpaṉikaḷ kaṇṭatuvum, 
pāviyaṟṟu taṟkup paḻakiyatum, paṇṭiyuḷa
nītinūl tērntatuvum nittam tamiḻkkalikaḷ
ōtiyatum, apporuḷi uḷḷattē vittatuvum,
centamiḻp pākkaṉikaḷ cērtteṉ cevippiḻintu
anta naṟuntēṉāl ānantam tantatuvum,
eppaṭittāṉ nīmaṟantāy, eṉṉaracu koḷpavaḷē!
tappitamēṉ ceytāy? takumō uṉakkitutāṉ?
eṉṉap palakūṟi ēṅkiṉāṉ maṉṉavaṉē.
araciyiṉ moḻi
aṉṉiyavaḷ taṇkōti āṟāta tuṉpamuṭaṉ,
uḷḷattik koḷḷikoḷum rōcā malarcintik
